## File: `app.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/app.py`

```python
import os
import sys
import shutil
import pathlib
import pprint
from PIL import Image

# Force UTF-8 encoding for console stdout
try:
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding='utf-8')
except Exception:
    pass

# Ensure the root path is in the sys path
root_path = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if root_path not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, root_path)

from workflow.langgraph_workflow import ProductionPipelineV2

def main():
    print("==================================================")
    print("Initializing Multi-Agent Pipeline (v2)...")
    pipeline = ProductionPipelineV2()
    
    # Check for uploaded images first in the uploads/ folder
    uploads_dir = pathlib.Path("uploads")
    images = []
    
    if uploads_dir.exists() and uploads_dir.is_dir():
        for path in uploads_dir.iterdir():
            if path.suffix.lower() in ('.png', '.jpg', '.jpeg', '.webp'):
                try:
                    img = Image.open(path)
                    images.append(img)
                    print(f"  Loaded uploaded image: {path.name} ({img.width}x{img.height} px)")
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"  Error loading upload {path.name}: {e}")

    # Fallback to default assets if uploads/ is empty
    if not images:
        fallback_paths = [
            "assets/front-view.png", 
            "assets/left-view.png", 
            "assets/right-view.png"
        ]
        for path in fallback_paths:
            p = pathlib.Path(path)
            if p.exists():
                img = Image.open(p)
                images.append(img)
                print(f"  Loaded local asset: {p.name} ({img.width}x{img.height} px)")
            else:
                print(f"  Fallback file not found at: {path}")

    # Fallback to creating a dummy image if assets folder is completely missing
    if not images:
        print("  Creating fallback dummy room image...")
        img = Image.new("RGB", (400, 400), color=(240, 240, 240))
        images.append(img)
    
    try:
        # Phase 0: User Preference Collection
        print("\n📥 Step 0: Collecting User Preferences...")
        user_prefs = {
            "required_products": [
                "study table",
                "office chair",
                "bookshelf"
            ],
            "budget_range": {
                "minimum": 10000,
                "maximum": 40000
            },
            "preferred_brands": [
                "IKEA",
                "Godrej Interio",
                "Green Soul"
            ],
            "excluded_brands": [],
            "preferred_style": "modern",
            "color_preferences": [
                "white",
                "oak wood"
            ],
            "priority": ["workspace", "budget"]
        }
        
        # Run Phase 1 - Phase 7 Production Pipeline v2
        print("\n🚀 Executing Production Pipeline v2 (Phase 1 to Phase 7)...")
        pipeline_output = pipeline.run_pipeline(
            images=images,
            user_preferences=user_prefs,
            room_type="bedroom",
            progress_callback=lambda step: print(f"  [Step] {step}")
        )
        
        session_id = pipeline_output["session_id"]
        session_path = pipeline_output["session_path"]
        
        print("\n✅ Multi-Agent Pipeline Analysis Complete!")
        print(f"Session ID: {session_id}")
        print(f"All outputs are stored in: {session_path}")
        
        # Display Recommended Products grouped by categories
        print("\n🛍️ Recommended Products by Categories:")
        for cat in user_prefs["required_products"]:
            normalized_key = cat.lower().replace(" ", "_")
            items = pipeline_output.get("commerce", {}).get(normalized_key, [])
            print(f"  * {cat.upper()}:")
            for idx, item in enumerate(items[:3]):
                print(f"    - {idx+1}. {item.get('brand')} - {item.get('name')} (Price: Rs. {item.get('price')}) | Style: {item.get('style')} | Score: {item.get('final_score')}")

        # Display Selected Products Costing & Budget compliance
        budget_info = pipeline_output.get("budget", {})
        print("\n💰 Budget Compliance Verification:")
        print(f"  Subtotal: Rs. {budget_info.get('subtotal')}")
        print(f"  Shipping: Rs. {budget_info.get('shipping')}")
        print(f"  Tax (GST): Rs. {budget_info.get('tax')}")
        print(f"  Total Cost: Rs. {budget_info.get('total')}")
        print(f"  Budget Limit: Rs. {budget_info.get('budget_limit')}")
        print(f"  Remaining Reserves: Rs. {budget_info.get('remaining_budget')}")

        # Display Reconstruction Options and FLUX prompts
        reconstruct_info = pipeline_output.get("reconstruction", {})
        print("\n🖼️ Reconstruction Layout Options (FLUX prompts):")
        for option_key in ["layout_1", "layout_2", "layout_3"]:
            opt = reconstruct_info.get(option_key, {})
            if opt:
                print(f"  * {opt.get('name')}:")
                for prompt in opt.get("prompts", []):
                    print(f"    - Prompt: \"{prompt}\"")
                print(f"    - Status: Collision {opt.get('validation', {}).get('collision')}, Path {opt.get('validation', {}).get('walking_path')}")
        
        print("\n--- Output Files Exported ---")
        print(f"1. Unified Scene Graph & Inventory: {session_path}/merged_output.json")
        print(f"2. Occupancy Grid matrix: {session_path}/occupancy_grid.json")
        print(f"3. Spatial Clearance Audit: {session_path}/spatial_analysis.json")
        print(f"4. Design Style Alignment: {session_path}/design_analysis.json")
        print(f"5. Product Recommendations: {session_path}/commerce_analysis.json")
        print(f"6. Budget Utilization profile: {session_path}/budget_analysis.json")
        print(f"7. Recommendation Rationale: {session_path}/explain_analysis.json")
        print(f"8. Layout Transition Delta: {session_path}/reconstruction_analysis.json")

        # Interactive notebook visualization block
        try:
            recon_img_path = pathlib.Path(session_path) / "processed_reconstructed.png"
            if recon_img_path.exists():
                print("\n🖼️ Rendering Reconstructed Room Layout visualization...")
                from IPython.display import display as ipy_display
                from PIL import Image as PILImage
                ipy_display(PILImage.open(recon_img_path))
        except Exception:
            pass
        
        # Removed hardcoded zipping
        pass
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n❌ Pipeline Execution Failed: {str(e)}")
        raise e
    finally:
        try:
            print("\n🧹 Reclaiming all VRAM resources...")
            from models.model_manager import ModelManager
            ModelManager().unload_model("vision")
            ModelManager().unload_model("reasoning")
            import torch
            import gc
            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
            gc.collect()
        except Exception:
            pass

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

import os

## File: `budget_agent.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/agents/budget_agent.py`

```python
"""Budget Agent Module for VisionSpace AI.

Responsible for calculating total cost, budget limits, remaining reserves,
and providing budget utilization metrics alongside dynamic alternatives.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import config
from models.text_model import TextModel
from utils.file_utils import save_json

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.BudgetAgent")


class BudgetAgent:
    """Production-grade BudgetAgent responsible for spending optimizations matching V2 schemas."""

    def __init__(self, provider: str = "huggingface") -> None:
        """Initializes the BudgetAgent."""
        self.provider_name = provider
        self.text_model = TextModel(provider=provider)
        logger.info("BudgetAgent initialized (Provider: %s)", provider)

    def analyze_budget(
        self,
        commerce_output: Dict[str, Any],
        budget_limit: float | int = 50000,
        session_path: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Runs the Phase 5 budget analysis engine."""
        logger.info("Running Phase 5 Budget Agent analysis...")

        # Accept either recommended_products list or commerce_output dictionary directly
        products = []
        if isinstance(commerce_output, list):
            products = commerce_output
        elif isinstance(commerce_output, dict):
            products = commerce_output.get("recommended_products") or []
        
        total_requested_cost = sum(float(p.get("price", 0)) for p in products)

        prompt_system = (
            "You are the Budget Agent of VisionSpace AI.\n"
            "Analyze item costs, calculate utilization metrics, and construct the budget profile.\n"
            "Strict JSON ONLY. No conversational intro/outro text. No explanations. No thinking tags."
        )

        prompt_user = (
            f"Cost Analysis:\n"
            f"- Selected Products: {products}\n"
            f"- Budget Limit: {budget_limit}\n"
            f"- Current Subtotal Cost: {total_requested_cost}\n\n"
            "Task:\n"
            "Format the total budget profile. Calculate subtotal, shipping, tax, total cost, and remaining budget.\n"
            "Return conforming JSON format:\n"
            "{\n"
            '  "subtotal": number,\n'
            '  "shipping": number,\n'
            '  "tax": number,\n'
            '  "total": number,\n'
            '  "budget_limit": number,\n'
            '  "remaining_budget": number\n'
            "}"
        )

        try:
            llm_res = self.text_model.generate(
                system_prompt=prompt_system,
                prompt=prompt_user,
                max_new_tokens=1024,
                temperature=0.1,
                enable_thinking=False  # Disabled for budget extraction tasks
            )
            raw_text = llm_res.get("text") if isinstance(llm_res, dict) else str(llm_res)
            llm_data = self._validate_and_repair_json(raw_text)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Budget LLM evaluation failed: %s", str(e))
            llm_data = {}

        # Fallback values if LLM fails
        fused = self._fuse_budget_payload(llm_data, total_requested_cost, budget_limit, products)

        # Save output if path is given
        if session_path is not None:
            try:
                import pathlib
                p = pathlib.Path(session_path)
                if p.is_dir():
                    save_json(fused, p / "budget_analysis.json")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("Failed to save budget analysis JSON: %s", str(e))

        return fused

    def _fuse_budget_payload(
        self, llm_data: Dict[str, Any], total_requested_cost: float, budget_limit: float, products: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        subtotal = float(llm_data.get("subtotal") if llm_data.get("subtotal") is not None else total_requested_cost)
        shipping = float(llm_data.get("shipping") if llm_data.get("shipping") is not None else 1200.0)
        tax = float(llm_data.get("tax") if llm_data.get("tax") is not None else round(subtotal * 0.10, 2))
        total = subtotal + shipping + tax
        b_limit = float(llm_data.get("budget_limit") if llm_data.get("budget_limit") is not None else budget_limit)
        remaining = b_limit - total

        return {
            "subtotal": subtotal,
            "shipping": shipping,
            "tax": tax,
            "total": total,
            "budget_limit": b_limit,
            "remaining_budget": remaining
        }

    def _validate_and_repair_json(self, raw_text: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Helper to repair and clean JSON response structures."""
        if not raw_text:
            return {}
        clean_text = raw_text.strip()
        
        # Locate outer JSON braces to discard extraneous reasoning text
        start_idx = clean_text.find("{")
        end_idx = clean_text.rfind("}")
        if start_idx != -1 and end_idx != -1 and end_idx > start_idx:
            clean_text = clean_text[start_idx:end_idx + 1].strip()
        
        # Remove code blocks if present
        if clean_text.startswith("```"):
            lines = clean_text.splitlines()
            if lines[0].startswith("```json") or lines[0].startswith("```"):
                clean_text = "\n".join(lines[1:-1]) if lines[-1].startswith("```") else "\n".join(lines[1:])
        
        clean_text = clean_text.strip()

        try:
            return json.loads(clean_text)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass

        try:
            repaired = re.sub(r',\s*([\]}])', r'\1', clean_text)
            return json.loads(repaired)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            from json_repair import repair_json
            repaired_lib = repair_json(clean_text, return_objects=True)
            if isinstance(repaired_lib, dict):
                return repaired_lib
        except Exception:
            pass

        return {}

```

## File: `commerce_agent.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/agents/commerce_agent.py`

```python
"""Commerce/Product Agent Module for VisionSpace AI.

Coordinates user preferences with room inventory, spatial clearances, and design styles
to output filtered product recommendations (requested products + complementary budget-aligned items).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import json
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import config
from models.text_model import TextModel
from utils.file_utils import save_json

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.CommerceAgent")

def extract_direct_link(link: str) -> str:
    """Extracts direct merchant link (IKEA, Amazon, Flipkart, etc.) from Google redirect URLs."""
    if not link:
        return ""
    if "google.com/url" in link or "google.adservices.com" in link:
        import urllib.parse
        try:
            parsed = urllib.parse.urlparse(link)
            params = urllib.parse.parse_qs(parsed.query)
            for key in ["adurl", "q", "url"]:
                if key in params and params[key]:
                    return params[key][0]
        except Exception:
            pass
    return link


class CommerceAgent:
    """Production-grade CommerceAgent responsible for item curation matching V2 schemas."""

        logger.info("CommerceAgent initialized (Provider: %s)", provider)

    def recommend_products(
        self,
        vision_output: Dict[str, Any],
        spatial_output: Dict[str, Any],
        design_output: Dict[str, Any],
        user_preferences: Dict[str, Any],
        session_path: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Orchestrates the Phase 4 recommendation pipeline matching conformed V2 structure."""
        logger.info("Running Phase 4 Product Agent recommendations...")

        # 1. Search products from catalog
        req_products = user_preferences.get("required_products") or []
        preferred_brands = user_preferences.get("preferred_brands") or []
        excluded_brands = user_preferences.get("excluded_brands") or []
        budget_range = user_preferences.get("budget_range") or {"minimum": 0, "maximum": 50000}
        max_budget = float(budget_range.get("maximum") or 50000.0)
        pref_style = user_preferences.get("preferred_style") or "modern"

        # Robust category matching helper function to handle variations like "queen-size bed" vs "bed"
        def is_category_match(item_cat: str, req_cat: str) -> bool:
            c1 = str(item_cat).lower()
            c2 = str(req_cat).lower()
            words = ["bed", "desk", "chair", "table", "bookshelf", "shelf", "fan", "ac", "conditioner", "projector", "screen", "lamp", "light"]
            for w in words:
                if w in c1 and w in c2:
                    return True
            return c1 == c2 or c2 in c1 or c1 in c2

        # Look up matching candidates in our catalog
        candidates = self.search_products(req_products, preferred_brands=preferred_brands)

        # Sort candidates to prioritize preferred brands first
        if preferred_brands and candidates:
            candidates = sorted(
                candidates,
                key=lambda x: x.get("brand", "").lower() in [b.lower() for b in preferred_brands],
                reverse=True
            )

        # 2. Apply filters (dimensions, budget, style, exclusions)
        candidates = self.filter_dimensions(candidates, max_w=150, max_d=80)
        
        # Calculate budget per item to fit the total budget limit
        num_items = len(req_products) if req_products else 1
        budget_per_item = max_budget / num_items if num_items > 1 else max_budget
        
        # Try to filter by budget_per_item first, falling back to max_budget if empty
        candidates_under_item_limit = [c for c in candidates if float(c.get("price", 0)) <= budget_per_item]
        
        # Check if we still have options for each required category
        has_all_cats = True
        for cat in req_products:
            normalized_key = cat.lower().replace(" ", "_")
            cat_found = False
            for c in candidates_under_item_limit:
                if (is_category_match(c.get("category", ""), cat) or 
                    is_category_match(c.get("name", ""), cat) or 
                    is_category_match(c.get("title", ""), cat)):
                    cat_found = True
                    break
            if not cat_found:
                has_all_cats = False
                break
                
        if has_all_cats:
            candidates = candidates_under_item_limit
        else:
            # Fall back to individual max_budget limit
            candidates = [c for c in candidates if float(c.get("price", 0)) <= max_budget]
        
        # Exclude brand filter
        candidates = [c for c in candidates if c.get("brand") not in excluded_brands]

        # 3. Score candidates using the conformed 5-factor formulas
        scored = []
        for c in candidates:
            scores = self.score_products(
                product=c,
                pref_style=pref_style,
                preferred_brands=preferred_brands,
                required_w=120,
                required_d=60,
                budget_limit=max_budget,
                spatial_output=spatial_output
            )
            item = dict(c)
            item.update(scores)
            # Add conformed schema score aliases matching Section 1
            item["fit_score"] = scores.get("fit_score", 0.8)
            item["fit_status"] = scores.get("fit_status", "unknown")
            item["design_score"] = scores.get("design_score", 0.8)
            item["rank_score"] = scores.get("final_score", 0.8)
            scored.append(item)

        # 4. Rank products
        ranked = self.rank_products(scored)
        
        # Keep top 10 per category first to prevent crowding out
        category_grouped = {}
        for cat in req_products:
            normalized_key = cat.lower().replace(" ", "_")
            cat_list = []
            for item in ranked:
                if (is_category_match(item.get("category", ""), cat) or 
                    is_category_match(item.get("name", ""), cat) or 
                    is_category_match(item.get("title", ""), cat)):
                    cat_list.append(item)
            category_grouped[normalized_key] = cat_list[:5] # Keep top 5 per category

        # Union top 3 of each category for the main recommended list.
        # To guarantee every required category is represented, we first add the #1 item of each category.
        curated_ranked = []
        added_ids = set()
        
        # Phase A: Add #1 item of each category
        for cat_list in category_grouped.values():
            if cat_list:
                item = cat_list[0]
                curated_ranked.append(item)
                added_ids.add(item.get("product_id"))
                
        # Phase B: Add subsequent items (up to top 3 per category) if not already added
        for cat_list in category_grouped.values():
            for item in cat_list[1:3]:
                if item.get("product_id") not in added_ids:
                    curated_ranked.append(item)
                    added_ids.add(item.get("product_id"))
                    
        # Sort the final curated list by rank score
        curated_ranked = self.rank_products(curated_ranked)

        # 5. Build shopping list & explanations
        shopping_list = self.build_shopping_list(curated_ranked)
        explanations = self.generate_explanations(curated_ranked)

        output = {
            "recommended_products": curated_ranked,
            "shopping_list": shopping_list,
            "explanations": explanations
        }

        # Populate category-specific outputs
        for k, v in category_grouped.items():
            output[k] = v

        # Save output if path is given
        if session_path is not None:
            try:
                import pathlib
                p = pathlib.Path(session_path)
                if p.is_dir():
                    save_json(output, p / "commerce_analysis.json")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("Failed to save commerce analysis JSON: %s", str(e))

        return output

    def search_products(self, categories: List[str], preferred_brands: Optional[List[str]] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Searches Google Shopping dynamically using SerpAPI if API key is provided, else falls back to mock catalog."""
        import config
        api_key = os.environ.get("SERP_API_KEY") or os.environ.get("SERPAPI_API_KEY") or getattr(config, "SERP_API", None)
        if not api_key:
            # Try UserSecretsClient if on Kaggle
            try:
                from kaggle_secrets import UserSecretsClient
                api_key = UserSecretsClient().get_secret("SERP_API_KEY") or UserSecretsClient().get_secret("SERPAPI_API_KEY")
            except BaseException:
                pass

        if not api_key:
            logger.warning("SerpAPI API key not found. Falling back to local catalog search.")
            return self._search_mock_catalog(categories)

        import urllib.request
        import urllib.parse
        import urllib.error
        
        results = []
        
        # Helper function to parse a single SerpAPI shopping item into conformed schema
        def parse_item(item, cat, detected_brand):
            title = item.get("title", "")
            price_str = item.get("price", "0")
            # Extract price digits
            price_cleaned = re.sub(r'[^\d.]', '', price_str)
            try:
                price = float(price_cleaned) if price_cleaned else 0.0
            except Exception:
                price = 0.0

            # Dynamic currency conversion to INR
            converted_price = price
            is_usd = False
            is_eur = False
            is_gbp = False
            is_cad = False
            
            price_str_lower = price_str.lower()
            currency_field = item.get("currency", "").upper()
            
            if "$" in price_str or "usd" in price_str_lower or currency_field == "USD":
                is_usd = True
            elif "€" in price_str or "eur" in price_str_lower or currency_field == "EUR":
                is_eur = True
            elif "£" in price_str or "gbp" in price_str_lower or currency_field == "GBP":
                is_gbp = True
            elif "c$" in price_str_lower or "cad" in price_str_lower or currency_field == "CAD":
                is_cad = True
                
            if is_usd:
                converted_price = round(price * 83.0, 2)
            elif is_eur:
                converted_price = round(price * 90.0, 2)
            elif is_gbp:
                converted_price = round(price * 105.0, 2)
            elif is_cad:
                converted_price = round(price * 61.0, 2)

            price = converted_price
            if price <= 0:
                price = 4500.0
                
            rating = float(item.get("rating", 4.0))
            quality_score = rating / 5.0 if rating > 0 else 0.8
            reviews_count = int(item.get("reviews", 50))
            image_url = item.get("thumbnail", "")
            
            # Parse dimensions from title
            w_cm, d_cm, h_cm = 120.0, 60.0, 75.0
            match = re.search(r'(\d+)\s*(?:x|by)\s*(\d+)', title.lower())
            if match:
                try:
                    w_cm = float(match.group(1))
                    d_cm = float(match.group(2))
                except Exception:
                    pass
                    
            # Build conformed product schema defined in Section 1
            product_id = item.get("product_id") or f"serp_{hash(title)%1000}"
            orig_link = item.get("product_link") or item.get("link") or item.get("serpapi_product_api") or ""
            if not orig_link or not (orig_link.startswith("http://") or orig_link.startswith("https://")):
                query_q = f"{detected_brand} {title}".strip()
                orig_link = f"https://www.google.com/search?tbm=shop&q={urllib.parse.quote(query_q)}"
            
            return {
                # Dynamic conformed Section 1 schema fields
                "product_id": product_id,
                "title": title,
                "brand": detected_brand,
                "price": price,
                "currency": "INR",
                "rating": rating,
                "reviews": reviews_count,
                "dimensions": {
                    "width_cm": w_cm,
                    "depth_cm": d_cm,
                    "height_cm": h_cm
                },
                "thumbnail_url": image_url,
                "high_resolution_image": image_url,
                "product_url": orig_link,
                "seller": detected_brand,
                "source": "serpapi",
                "marketplace": detected_brand,
                "availability": True,
                "shipping_cost": 599,
                "installation_cost": 0,
                
                # Backwards-compatibility legacy properties
                "name": title,
                "category": cat,
                "style": "modern",
                "width_cm": w_cm,
                "depth_cm": d_cm,
                "quality_score": round(quality_score, 2),
                "image": image_url,
                "link": orig_link
            }

        # Process each category independently to ensure we never truncate entire categories!
        for cat in categories or ["desk"]:
            branded_results = []
            fallback_results = []
            
            # Step A: Try searching with preferred brands first
            if preferred_brands:
                for brand in preferred_brands[:2]: # Limit to top 2 brands to avoid query limit hits
                    query = f"{brand} {cat}".strip()
                    max_retries = 2
                    import time
                    for attempt in range(max_retries):
                        try:
                            logger.info("Executing branded SerpAPI query (Attempt %d/%d): '%s'", attempt+1, max_retries, query)
                            query_encoded = urllib.parse.quote(query)
                            url = f"https://serpapi.com/search.json?engine=google_shopping&q={query_encoded}&api_key={api_key}"
                            
                            req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
                            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4) as response:
                                data = json.loads(response.read().decode('utf-8'))
                                shopping_results = data.get("shopping_results", [])
                                
                                for item in shopping_results:
                                    parsed = parse_item(item, cat, brand)
                                    if parsed:
                                        branded_results.append(parsed)
                            break # Success, exit retry loop
                        except Exception as e:
                            logger.warning("Branded query failed (Attempt %d/%d) for '%s': %s", attempt+1, max_retries, query, str(e))
                            if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                                time.sleep(1.0)
                        
            # Step B: Generic search fallback
            # If branded search returned 0 items, or if no preferred brands are set:
            # We run a generic category search to ensure we ALWAYS return products of ANY brand!
            if not branded_results:
                query = cat
                max_retries = 2
                import time
                for attempt in range(max_retries):
                    try:
                        logger.info("Executing generic fallback SerpAPI query (Attempt %d/%d): '%s'", attempt+1, max_retries, query)
                        query_encoded = urllib.parse.quote(query)
                        url = f"https://serpapi.com/search.json?engine=google_shopping&q={query_encoded}&api_key={api_key}"
                        
                        req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers={'User-Agent': 'Mozilla/5.0'})
                        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=4) as response:
                            data = json.loads(response.read().decode('utf-8'))
                            shopping_results = data.get("shopping_results", [])
                            
                            for item in shopping_results:
                                source = item.get("source", "")
                                detected_brand = item.get("brand") or source or "Generic"
                                parsed = parse_item(item, cat, detected_brand)
                                if parsed:
                                    fallback_results.append(parsed)
                        break # Success, exit retry loop
                    except Exception as e:
                        logger.warning("Generic query failed (Attempt %d/%d) for '%s': %s", attempt+1, max_retries, query, str(e))
                        if attempt < max_retries - 1:
                            time.sleep(1.0)
            
            cat_results = branded_results if branded_results else fallback_results
            
            # Step C: Category fallback check if live results are still empty
            if not cat_results:
                logger.info("SerpAPI failed completely for category '%s'. Fetching fallbacks from local mock catalog...", cat)
                try:
                    mock_items = self._search_mock_catalog([cat])
                    cat_results.extend(mock_items)
                except Exception as mock_err:
                    logger.error("Failed to fetch mock fallbacks for category '%s': %s", cat, str(mock_err))
                
            results.extend(cat_results)
            
        return results

    def _search_mock_catalog(self, categories: List[str]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Fallback search logic using local mock catalog."""
        if not categories:
            matches = [c for c in self.mock_catalog if c.get("category") == "desk"]
        else:
            matches = []
            for cat in categories:
                normalized_cat = cat.lower()
                words = ["bed", "desk", "chair", "table", "bookshelf", "shelf", "fan", "ac", "conditioner", "projector", "screen", "lamp", "light", "cooler", "coffee", "laptop", "holder", "stand", "maker"]
                match_words = [w for w in words if w in normalized_cat]
                
                cat_matches = []
                for c in self.mock_catalog:
                    item_cat = c.get("category", "").lower()
                    item_name = c.get("name", "").lower()
                    matched = False
                    if match_words:
                        for w in match_words:
                            if w in item_cat or w in item_name:
                                matched = True
                                break
                    if not matched and (normalized_cat in item_cat or normalized_cat in item_name or item_cat in normalized_cat):
                        matched = True
                    if matched:
                        cat_matches.append(c)
                matches.extend(cat_matches)
        
        if not matches:
            matches = list(self.mock_catalog)

        # Enrich with reviews, rating, image, link conformed to Section 1 schema
        enriched = []
        for idx, item in enumerate(matches):
            rating = item.get("rating", round(item.get("quality_score", 0.8) * 5.0, 1))
            reviews = item.get("reviews", 50)
            
            # Resolve a beautiful, high-fidelity Unsplash product image based on the item's category
            cat_lower = item.get("category", "").lower()
            if "desk" in cat_lower or "table" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1518455027359-f3f8164ba6bd?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "chair" in cat_lower or "stool" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1505797149-43b0069ec26b?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "bookshelf" in cat_lower or "shelf" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1594980596870-8cb524ebf6bc?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "bed" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1505693416388-ac5ce068fe85?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "ac" in cat_lower or "conditioner" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1585338107529-13afc5f02586?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "projector" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1535016120720-40c646be5580?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "lighting" in cat_lower or "lamp" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1507473885765-e6ed057f782c?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            elif "cabinet" in cat_lower or "wardrobe" in cat_lower:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1595428774223-ef52624120d2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
            else:
                img_url = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1513151233558-d860c5398176?auto=format&fit=crop&w=150&q=80"
                
            thumbnail_url = item.get("image") or img_url
            high_res = thumbnail_url.replace("w=150", "w=800") if "unsplash.com" in thumbnail_url else thumbnail_url
            
            p_url = item.get("link")
            if not p_url:
                import urllib.parse
                search_q = urllib.parse.quote(f"{item.get('brand')} {item.get('name')}")
                p_url = f"https://www.google.com/search?tbm=shop&q={search_q}"
                
            w_cm = float(item.get("width_cm", 120))
            d_cm = float(item.get("depth_cm", 60))
            h_cm = float(item.get("height_cm", 75))
            
            conformed_product = {
                # Dynamic conformed Section 1 schema fields
                "product_id": f"mock_{idx + 1:03d}",
                "title": item.get("name"),
                "brand": item.get("brand"),
                "price": float(item.get("price", 0)),
                "currency": "INR",
                "rating": float(rating),
                "reviews": int(reviews),
                "dimensions": {
                    "width_cm": w_cm,
                    "depth_cm": d_cm,
                    "height_cm": h_cm
                },
                "thumbnail_url": thumbnail_url,
                "high_resolution_image": high_res,
                "product_url": p_url,
                "seller": item.get("brand"),
                "source": "mock_catalog",
                "marketplace": item.get("brand"),
                "availability": True,
                "shipping_cost": 0,
                "installation_cost": 0,
                
                # Backwards-compatibility legacy properties
                "name": item.get("name"),
                "category": item.get("category"),
                "style": item.get("style", "modern"),
                "width_cm": w_cm,
                "depth_cm": d_cm,
                "quality_score": float(item.get("quality_score", 0.8)),
                "image": thumbnail_url,
                "link": p_url
            }
            enriched.append(conformed_product)
            
        return enriched

    def filter_dimensions(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]], max_w: float, max_d: float) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Filters out items that exceed the available room clearance boundaries."""
        filtered = []
        for p in products:
            w = float(p.get("width_cm", 0))
            d = float(p.get("depth_cm", 0))
            cat_lower = p.get("category", "").lower()
            
            # Set category-specific dimension boundaries to prevent false filtering of large items (Beds/Screens)
            limit_w = max_w
            limit_d = max_d
            
            if "bed" in cat_lower:
                limit_w = 250.0
                limit_d = 220.0
            elif "screen" in cat_lower or "tv" in cat_lower:
                limit_w = 300.0
                limit_d = 100.0
            elif "projector" in cat_lower or "ac" in cat_lower or "conditioner" in cat_lower or "lighting" in cat_lower or "fan" in cat_lower:
                limit_w = 200.0
                limit_d = 200.0
                
            if w <= limit_w and d <= limit_d:
                filtered.append(p)
        return filtered

    def filter_budget(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]], budget_limit: float) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Filters out items that exceed the target budget range."""
        return [p for p in products if float(p.get("price", 0)) <= budget_limit]

    def filter_style(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]], style: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Filters/prioritizes items matching the theme styling."""
        # We don't discard non-matching styles unless they clash, so we keep them but style scoring handles prioritizing
        return products

    def score_products(
        self,
        product: Dict[str, Any],
        pref_style: str,
        preferred_brands: List[str],
        required_w: float,
        required_d: float,
        budget_limit: float,
        spatial_output: Dict[str, Any] = None
    ) -> Dict[str, float]:
        """Calculates conformed weighted score for the product candidate.

        Formula:
          Final Score = 0.40 * fit_score + 0.25 * rating_score + 0.15 * design_score + 0.10 * brand_score + 0.10 * popularity_score
        """
        # 1. fit_score: how well dimensions fit the empty regions/clearance boundaries (40%)
        prod_w = float(product.get("width_cm") or required_w)
        prod_d = float(product.get("depth_cm") or required_d)
        prod_h = float(product.get("height_cm") or 60.0)
        
        # Determine room boundaries if available
        room_w = 400.0
        room_d = 500.0
        if spatial_output and "room_dimensions_cm" in spatial_output:
            room_w = float(spatial_output["room_dimensions_cm"].get("width", 400.0))
            room_d = float(spatial_output["room_dimensions_cm"].get("length", 500.0))

        # Classify placement domain using the reusable ProductClassifier
        from utils.product_classifier import ProductClassifier
        canonical_category, placement_domain = ProductClassifier.classify(product)
        
        # Enrich candidate product metadata (Section 6)
        product["canonical_category"] = canonical_category
        product["placement_domain"] = placement_domain
        
        domain = "floor"
        if placement_domain == "ceiling":
            domain = "ceiling"
        elif placement_domain == "wall":
            domain = "wall"
        elif placement_domain in ["support_surface", "surface"]:
            domain = "surface"
        elif placement_domain == "wall_or_surface":
            domain = "wall"
        elif placement_domain == "ceiling_or_surface":
            domain = "surface"

        fit_status = "unknown"
        fit_score = 0.5

        if spatial_output:
            if domain == "ceiling":
                ceilings = spatial_output.get("candidate_ceiling_regions", [])
                if ceilings:
                    fit_status = "no_space"
                    fit_score = 0.1
                    for c in ceilings:
                        cw = float(c.get("width_cm") or room_w)
                        cd = float(c.get("depth_cm") or room_d)
                        if prod_w <= cw and prod_d <= cd:
                            fit_status = "fits"
                            fit_score = 1.0
                            break
                else:
                    fit_status = "fits"
                    fit_score = 0.9
                    
            elif domain == "wall":
                walls = spatial_output.get("candidate_wall_regions", [])
                if walls:
                    fit_status = "no_space"
                    fit_score = 0.1
                    for w_seg in walls:
                        ww = float(w_seg.get("width_cm", room_w))
                        wh = float(w_seg.get("height_cm", 290.0))
                        if prod_w <= ww and prod_h <= wh:
                            fit_status = "fits"
                            fit_score = 1.0
                            break
                else:
                    fit_status = "fits"
                    fit_score = 0.9
                    
            elif domain == "surface":
                surfaces = spatial_output.get("support_surfaces", [])
                if surfaces:
                    fit_status = "no_space"
                    fit_score = 0.1
                    for s in surfaces:
                        s_polygon = s.get("footprint_polygon", [])
                        if s_polygon:
                            sw = max(pt[0] for pt in s_polygon) - min(pt[0] for pt in s_polygon)
                            sd = max(pt[1] for pt in s_polygon) - min(pt[1] for pt in s_polygon)
                            if prod_w <= sw and prod_d <= sd:
                                fit_status = "fits"
                                fit_score = 1.0
                                break
                        else:
                            fit_status = "fits"
                            fit_score = 1.0
                            break
                else:
                    fit_status = "no_space"
                    fit_score = 0.0 # No surface exists to support accessories!
                    
            elif domain == "floor":
                floors = spatial_output.get("candidate_floor_regions", [])
                if not floors:
                    floors = spatial_output.get("empty_regions", [])
                
                if floors:
                    fit_status = "no_space"
                    fit_score = 0.0
                    for f in floors:
                        fw = float(f.get("width_cm") or 0.0)
                        fd = float(f.get("depth_cm") or 0.0)
                        if prod_w <= fw and prod_d <= fd:
                            fit_status = "fits"
                            fit_score = 1.0
                            break
                        elif prod_w <= fw + 20.0 and prod_d <= fd + 20.0:
                            fit_status = "tight_fit"
                            fit_score = 0.7
                else:
                    fit_status = "unknown"
                    fit_score = 0.5
        else:
            fit_status = "fits" if (prod_w <= required_w and prod_d <= required_d) else "no_space"
            fit_score = 1.0 if fit_status == "fits" else 0.2

        # 2. rating_score (25%)
        rating = float(product.get("rating") or product.get("quality_score", 0.8) * 5.0 or 4.0)
        rating_score = min(1.0, max(0.0, rating / 5.0))

        # 3. design_score: style compatibility (15%)
        style_match = product.get("style", "").lower() == pref_style.lower()
        design_score = 1.0 if style_match else 0.6

        # 4. brand_score: brand preference (10%)
        brand_match = product.get("brand", "").lower() in [b.lower() for b in preferred_brands]
        brand_score = 1.0 if brand_match else 0.5

        # 5. popularity_score: based on reviews count or fallback (10%)
        reviews_count = int(product.get("reviews", 50))
        popularity_score = min(1.0, reviews_count / 100.0)

        # Final weighted score
        final_score = (
            0.40 * fit_score +
            0.25 * rating_score +
            0.15 * design_score +
            0.10 * brand_score +
            0.10 * popularity_score
        )
        if fit_status == "no_space":
            final_score -= 1.0 # Ensure no_space items rank at the very bottom
        
        return {
            "fit_score": round(fit_score, 2),
            "fit_status": fit_status,
            "rating_score": round(rating_score, 2),
            "design_score": round(design_score, 2),
            "brand_score": round(brand_score, 2),
            "popularity_score": round(popularity_score, 2),
            "final_score": round(final_score, 3)
        }

    def rank_products(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Sorts candidate products in descending order based on their weighted score."""
        return sorted(products, key=lambda x: x.get("final_score", 0.0), reverse=True)

    def generate_explanations(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[str]:
        """Generates clear, concise justifications for product selections."""
        explanations = []
        for p in products[:3]:
            explanations.append(
                f"Selected {p.get('name')} (Score: {p.get('final_score')}) because it fits clearance "
                f"envelopes and matches styling parameters within budget limits."
            )
        return explanations

    def build_shopping_list(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Prepares a shopping catalog of selected items."""
        shopping_list = []
        for p in products:
            shopping_list.append({
                "item": p.get("name"),
                "category": p.get("category"),
                "brand": p.get("brand"),
                "price": p.get("price")
            })
        return shopping_list

    def generate_visual_preview(self, products: List[Dict[str, Any]], session_path: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Generates a visualization preview manifest utilizing FLUX Kontext for reconstruction."""
        logger.info("Generating FLUX Kontext reconstruction preview parameters...")
        
        prompts = []
        for p in products[:2]:
            prompts.append(
                f"Add a {p.get('style')} {p.get('brand')} {p.get('category')} to the room floor space, "
                "preserving exact room perspective and ambient lighting."
            )

        preview_manifest = {
            "reconstruction_model": "FLUX Kontext",
            "reasoning": "Preserves room layout identity and geometric boundaries better than SDXL.",
            "prompts": prompts,
            "base_image": "original_1.png",
            "output_preview_image": "processed_reconstructed.png"
        }

        try:
            import pathlib
            p_path = pathlib.Path(session_path)
            if p_path.is_dir():
                save_json(preview_manifest, p_path / "reconstruction_preview.json")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Failed to save reconstruction preview: %s", str(e))

        return preview_manifest

```

## File: `design_agent.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/agents/design_agent.py`

```python
"""Design Agent Module for VisionSpace AI.

Analyzes scene graphs, layout metrics, color presets, brand preferences,
and custom constraints to generate an optimized interior design plan matching the master prompt.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import config
from models.text_model import TextModel
from utils.prompt_templates import design_recommendation_prompt
from utils.file_utils import save_json

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.DesignAgent")


class DesignAgent:
    """Production-grade DesignAgent responsible for style alignment and design plan optimizations."""

    def __init__(self, provider: str = "huggingface") -> None:
        """Initializes the DesignAgent.

        Args:
            provider: Inference provider ("huggingface").
        """
        self.provider_name = provider
        self.text_model = TextModel(provider=provider)
        logger.info("DesignAgent initialized (Provider: %s)", provider)

    def generate_recommendations(
        self,
        vision_output: Dict[str, Any],
        spatial_output: Dict[str, Any],
        budget: float | int = 50000,
        preferred_style: str = "scandinavian",
        preferred_colors: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        preferred_brands: Optional[List[str]] = None,
        constraints: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None,
        session_path: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Orchestrates design plan recommendations generation."""
        logger.info("Generating style alignment and design recommendations...")

        # Extract merged scene if nested in a VisionAgent response structure
        if "merged_scene" in vision_output:
            vision_output = vision_output["merged_scene"]

        walkability_score = spatial_output.get("walkability_score", 100)
        design_mode = "space_constrained" if walkability_score < 60 else "standard"
        
        prompt_system = (
            "You are the Design Agent of VisionSpace AI. Perform interior design evaluations.\n"
            "Analyze current styling, colors, lighting, and layout opportunities.\n"
            "Strict JSON ONLY. No conversational intro/outro text. No explanations. No thinking tags in output JSON."
        )
        prompt_user = (
            f"Current room details:\n"
            f"- Room Type: {vision_output.get('room_type', 'bedroom')}\n"
            f"- Empty regions: {vision_output.get('empty_regions', [])}\n"
            f"- Spatial walkability: {walkability_score}\n"
            f"- Design Mode: {design_mode}\n\n"
            "Analyze the design aesthetics and return conforming JSON:\n"
            "{\n"
            '  "design_theme": "string (e.g. modern minimal)",\n'
            '  "wall_color": "string",\n'
            '  "floor_material": "string",\n'
            '  "lighting_type": "string (e.g. warm lighting)",\n'
            '  "dominant_colors": ["hex_colors"],\n'
            '  "style_score": number,\n'
            '  "recommended_additions": [\n'
            '    {\n'
            '      "item_type": "string (e.g. bookcase, table, chair, ceiling_fan, projector, coffee_maker)",\n'
            '      "priority": "recommended | required",\n'
            '      "preferred_zone": {\n'
            '        "zone_type": "wall_adjacent | corner | ceiling_center | support_surface",\n'
            '        "wall_id": "wall_left | wall_right | wall_front | wall_back | none",\n'
            '        "reason": "string"\n'
            '      },\n'
            '      "placement_constraints": {\n'
            '        "must_be_floor_supported": boolean,\n'
            '        "must_be_wall_adjacent": boolean,\n'
            '        "avoid_window": boolean,\n'
            '        "avoid_door": boolean,\n'
            '        "minimum_front_clearance_cm": number\n'
            '      }\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  ],\n'
            '  "rejected_requests": [\n'
            '    {\n'
            '      "item": "string (e.g. large bookshelf)",\n'
            '      "reason": "string"\n'
            '    }\n'
            '  ]\n'
            "}"
        )

        try:
            llm_res = self.text_model.generate(
                system_prompt=prompt_system,
                prompt=prompt_user,
                max_new_tokens=1024,
                temperature=0.1,
                enable_thinking=True
            )
            raw_text = llm_res.get("text") if isinstance(llm_res, dict) else str(llm_res)
            llm_data = self._validate_and_repair_json(raw_text)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("LLM Design generation failed: %s", str(e))
            llm_data = {}

        # Fuses final output payload
        final_output = self._fuse_design_payload(llm_data, preferred_style, budget, design_mode)

        # Save output if path is given
        if session_path is not None:
            try:
                import pathlib
                p = pathlib.Path(session_path)
                if p.is_dir():
                    save_json(final_output, p / "design_analysis.json")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("Failed to save design analysis JSON: %s", str(e))

        return final_output

    def _validate_and_repair_json(self, raw_text: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Helper to repair and clean JSON response structures."""
        if not raw_text:
            return {}
        clean_text = raw_text.strip()
        
        # Strip thinking block (<think>...</think>) if present
        if "<think>" in clean_text.lower():
            if "</think>" in clean_text.lower():
                parts = clean_text.split("</think>")
                clean_text = parts[-1].strip()
            else:
                idx = clean_text.lower().find("<think>")
                brace_idx = clean_text.find("{", idx)
                if brace_idx != -1:
                    clean_text = clean_text[brace_idx:].strip()
                else:
                    clean_text = clean_text[idx + len("<think>"):].strip()
        
        # Locate outer JSON braces to discard extraneous reasoning text
        start_idx = clean_text.find("{")
        end_idx = clean_text.rfind("}")
        if start_idx != -1 and end_idx != -1 and end_idx > start_idx:
            clean_text = clean_text[start_idx:end_idx + 1].strip()
        
        # Remove code blocks if present
        if clean_text.startswith("```"):
            lines = clean_text.splitlines()
            if lines[0].startswith("```json") or lines[0].startswith("```"):
                clean_text = "\n".join(lines[1:-1]) if lines[-1].startswith("```") else "\n".join(lines[1:])
        
        clean_text = clean_text.strip()

        try:
            return json.loads(clean_text)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass

        # Strategy 2: Remove trailing commas
        try:
            repaired = re.sub(r',\s*([\]}])', r'\1', clean_text)
            return json.loads(repaired)
        except Exception:
            pass

        # Strategy 3: json-repair fallback
        try:
            from json_repair import repair_json
            repaired_lib = repair_json(clean_text, return_objects=True)
            if isinstance(repaired_lib, dict):
                return repaired_lib
        except Exception:
            pass

        logger.error("Design JSON parsing completely failed. returning empty dict.")
        return {}

    def _get_fallback_design_json(self, style: str, budget: float | int) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Backup design plan structure if LLM inference crashed."""
        return {
            "design_theme": f"Warm {style.capitalize()} Sanctuary",
            "design_style": style,
            "room_goal": "Optimize layout for ergonomic productivity, accessibility, and visual balance.",
            "materials": {
                "primary_material": "Oak Wood",
                "secondary_material": "Linen Fabric",
                "accent_material": "Black Powder Coated Steel"
            },
            "lighting_strategy": {
                "lighting_mood": "Cozy, layered, warm white tone for reading and working.",
                "color_temperature": "3000K",
                "natural_light_strategy": "Desk placed next to window for direct lighting; light linen curtains to reduce glare."
            },
            "color_palette": {
                "primary_colors": ["White", "Oak"],
                "secondary_colors": ["Light Grey"],
                "accent_colors": ["Sage Green"],
                "wall_colors": ["Off-White"],
                "furniture_colors": ["Oak", "White"]
            },
            "recommended_furniture": [
                {
                    "id": "desk_001",
                    "name": "Oak Study Desk",
                    "category": "desk",
                    "priority": "Mandatory",
                    "estimated_cost": int(budget * 0.15),
                    "retain_existing": False,
                    "reason": "Provides a dedicated ergonomically aligned workspace."
                }
            ],
            "layout_plan": [
                {
                    "furniture_id": "desk_001",
                    "position": {
                        "x_cm": 150,
                        "y_cm": 200
                    },
                    "rotation": 90,
                    "zone": "workspace",
                    "clearance_valid": True
                }
            ],
            "functional_zones": ["sleeping", "working", "storage", "circulation"],
            "budget_breakdown": {
                "furniture": int(budget * 0.6),
                "lighting": int(budget * 0.1),
                "decor": int(budget * 0.05),
                "storage": int(budget * 0.15),
                "buffer": int(budget * 0.1),
                "total": int(budget)
            },
            "shopping_requirements": [
                {
                    "category": "desk",
                    "required_width_cm": 120,
                    "required_depth_cm": 60,
                    "style": style,
                    "material": "oak",
                    "budget_limit": int(budget * 0.15),
                    "preferred_brands": ["IKEA", "Urban Ladder"]
                }
            ],
            "estimated_total_cost": int(budget * 0.9),
            "design_explanations": [
                "The Oak Study Desk was selected to create a functional work-from-home zone.",
                "It was placed next to the window to maximize natural light.",
                "We preserved a clear 90 cm wheelchair accessible walking corridor from the entrance."
            ],
            "confidence_score": 0.85
        }

    def _fuse_design_payload(self, llm_data: Dict[str, Any], style: str, budget: float | int, design_mode: str = "standard") -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Conforms output structure strictly to Design Agent schema."""
        default_additions = [
            {
                "item_type": "study table",
                "priority": "required",
                "preferred_zone": {
                    "zone_type": "wall_adjacent",
                    "wall_id": "wall_left",
                    "reason": "Workspace next to left wall."
                },
                "placement_constraints": {
                    "must_be_floor_supported": True,
                    "must_be_wall_adjacent": True,
                    "avoid_window": True,
                    "avoid_door": True,
                    "minimum_front_clearance_cm": 45 if design_mode == "space_constrained" else 60
                }
            },
            {
                "item_type": "bookshelf",
                "priority": "recommended",
                "preferred_zone": {
                    "zone_type": "wall_adjacent",
                    "wall_id": "wall_back",
                    "reason": "Vertical storage on rear wall."
                },
                "placement_constraints": {
                    "must_be_floor_supported": True,
                    "must_be_wall_adjacent": True,
                    "avoid_window": True,
                    "avoid_door": True,
                    "minimum_front_clearance_cm": 30 if design_mode == "space_constrained" else 45
                }
            },
            {
                "item_type": "ceiling fan",
                "priority": "recommended",
                "preferred_zone": {
                    "zone_type": "ceiling_center",
                    "wall_id": "none",
                    "reason": "Central placement on ceiling."
                },
                "placement_constraints": {
                    "must_be_floor_supported": False,
                    "must_be_wall_adjacent": False,
                    "avoid_window": False,
                    "avoid_door": False,
                    "minimum_front_clearance_cm": 0
                }
            }
        ]
        
        default_rejected = []
        if design_mode == "space_constrained":
            default_rejected = [
                {
                    "item": "large bookshelf",
                    "reason": "Insufficient floor clearance in space-constrained room."
                },
                {
                    "item": "large sofa",
                    "reason": "Exceeds available room footprint."
                }
            ]

        recommended_additions = llm_data.get("recommended_additions") or default_additions
        rejected_requests = llm_data.get("rejected_requests") or default_rejected
        
        return {
            "design_mode": design_mode,
            "design_theme": str(llm_data.get("design_theme") or f"modern {style}"),
            "wall_color": str(llm_data.get("wall_color") or "warm white"),
            "floor_material": str(llm_data.get("floor_material") or "oak wood"),
            "lighting_type": str(llm_data.get("lighting_type") or "warm lighting"),
            "dominant_colors": list(llm_data.get("dominant_colors") or ["#ffffff", "#c49a6c"]),
            "style_score": float(llm_data.get("style_score") if llm_data.get("style_score") is not None else 0.91),
            "recommended_additions": recommended_additions,
            "rejected_requests": rejected_requests
        }

```

## File: `explain_agent.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/agents/explain_agent.py`

```python
"""Explanation Agent Module for VisionSpace AI.

Responsible for generating human-readable layout, budget, and product
recommendation justifications conformed to word count constraints.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

import config
from models.text_model import TextModel
from utils.file_utils import save_json

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.ExplanationAgent")


class ExplanationAgent:
    """Production-grade ExplanationAgent responsible for qualitative rationale generation matching V2 schemas."""

    def __init__(self, provider: str = "huggingface") -> None:
        """Initializes the ExplanationAgent."""
        self.provider_name = provider
        self.text_model = TextModel(provider=provider)
        logger.info("ExplanationAgent initialized (Provider: %s)", provider)

    def generate_explanations(
        self,
        vision_output: Dict[str, Any],
        spatial_output: Dict[str, Any],
        design_output: Dict[str, Any],
        commerce_output: Dict[str, Any],
        budget_output: Dict[str, Any],
        session_path: Optional[Any] = None,
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Runs the Phase 6 explanation engine."""
        logger.info("Running Phase 6 Explanation Agent justifications...")

        products = commerce_output.get("recommended_products") or []
        budget_limit = budget_output.get("budget_limit", 50000)
        total_cost = budget_output.get("total_cost", 0)

        prompt_system = (
            "You are the Explanation Agent of VisionSpace AI. Generate structured explanations.\n"
            "Analyze layouts, selections, and budget parameters.\n"
            "Word limit: Under 50 words per product explanation.\n"
            "Strict JSON ONLY. No conversational intro/outro text. No explanations. No thinking tags in output JSON."
        )

        prompt_user = (
            f"Context details:\n"
            f"- Recommended Products: {products}\n"
            f"- Usable Space: {spatial_output.get('usable_area', 7.2)} sqm\n"
            f"- Walkable Space: {spatial_output.get('walkable_area', 5.4)} sqm\n"
            f"- Design Theme: {design_output.get('design_theme', 'modern minimal')}\n"
            f"- Budget Status: {budget_output.get('subtotal', total_cost)} subtotal, limit: {budget_limit}\n\n"
            "Task:\n"
            "Generate short explanations justifying the selection of each product (why it fits and why it was chosen), "
            "and why the placement location was selected (e.g. desk placed near window for natural light, chair selected because width fits free area).\n"
            "Do not exceed 50 words per product explanation!\n"
            "Return conforming JSON format:\n"
            "{\n"
            '  "product_explanations": ["list of strings justifying selected items"],\n'
            '  "placement_explanations": ["list of strings justifying why objects were located in specific regions"],\n'
            '  "budget_explanations": ["list of strings detailing budget alignment/optimizations"]\n'
            "}"
        )

        try:
            llm_res = self.text_model.generate(
                system_prompt=prompt_system,
                prompt=prompt_user,
                max_new_tokens=1024,
                temperature=0.1,
                enable_thinking=True  # Enabled for qualitative reasoning tasks
            )
            raw_text = llm_res.get("text") if isinstance(llm_res, dict) else str(llm_res)
            llm_data = self._validate_and_repair_json(raw_text)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Explanation LLM evaluation failed: %s", str(e))
            llm_data = {}

        # Fallback values if LLM fails
        fused = self._fuse_explanation_payload(llm_data, products)

        # Save output if path is given
        if session_path is not None:
            try:
                import pathlib
                p = pathlib.Path(session_path)
                if p.is_dir():
                    save_json(fused, p / "explain_analysis.json")
            except Exception as e:
                logger.error("Failed to save explain analysis JSON: %s", str(e))

        return fused

    def _fuse_explanation_payload(
        self, llm_data: Dict[str, Any], products: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        fused = {}
        
        pe = llm_data.get("product_explanations")
        if not pe or not isinstance(pe, list):
            pe = []
            for p in products:
                pe.append(f"The {p.get('name', 'item')} is selected because it fits the modern style and fulfills the user functional requirement.")
        fused["product_explanations"] = pe

        ple = llm_data.get("placement_explanations")
        if not ple or not isinstance(ple, list):
            ple = ["The desk is placed next to the window to maximize natural light usage while leaving the central path clear."]
        fused["placement_explanations"] = ple

        be = llm_data.get("budget_explanations")
        if not be or not isinstance(be, list):
            be = ["All items stay within boundaries, achieving optimal utility and style within the budget constraint."]
        fused["budget_explanations"] = be

        return fused

    def _validate_and_repair_json(self, raw_text: Optional[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Helper to repair and clean JSON response structures."""
        if not raw_text:
            return {}
        clean_text = raw_text.strip()
        
        # Locate outer JSON braces to discard extraneous reasoning text
        start_idx = clean_text.find("{")
        end_idx = clean_text.rfind("}")
        if start_idx != -1 and end_idx != -1 and end_idx > start_idx:
            clean_text = clean_text[start_idx:end_idx + 1].strip()
        
        # Remove code blocks if present
        if clean_text.startswith("```"):
            lines = clean_text.splitlines()
            if lines[0].startswith("```json") or lines[0].startswith("```"):
                clean_text = "\n".join(lines[1:-1]) if lines[-1].startswith("```") else "\n".join(lines[1:])
        
        clean_text = clean_text.strip()

        try:
            return json.loads(clean_text)
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass

        try:
            repaired = re.sub(r',\s*([\]}])', r'\1', clean_text)
            return json.loads(repaired)
        except Exception:
            pass

        try:
            from json_repair import repair_json
            repaired_lib = repair_json(clean_text, return_objects=True)
            if isinstance(repaired_lib, dict):
                return repaired_lib
        except Exception:
            pass

        return {}

```

## File: `reconstruction_agent.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/agents/reconstruction_agent.py`

```python
"""Reconstruction Agent Module for VisionSpace AI.

Coordinates final layout state transition reports, removing replaced elements
and inserting selected products while maintaining dimensions and geometry constraints.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

from utils.file_utils import save_json

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.ReconstructionAgent")


def apply_edge_feathering(img: Any, feather_radius: int = 15) -> Any:
    """Applies soft edge feathering to a PIL image to blend it naturally into the room snapshot."""
    from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
    img = img.convert("RGBA")
    width, height = img.size
    
    # Create mask channel
    mask = Image.new("L", (width, height), 255)
    draw_mask = ImageDraw.Draw(mask)
    
    # Draw a soft feathered gradient from edges
    for i in range(feather_radius):
        alpha = int(255 * (i / feather_radius))
        # Draw nested hollow rectangles with increasing alpha transparency
        draw_mask.rectangle(
            [i, i, width - 1 - i, height - 1 - i],
            fill=None,
            outline=alpha
        )
        
    alpha_channel = img.split()[3]
    from PIL import ImageChops
    blended_alpha = ImageChops.darker(alpha_channel, mask)
    img.putalpha(blended_alpha)
    return img


def make_background_transparent(img: Any) -> Any:
    """Converts solid or near-solid backgrounds of product thumbnails to transparent."""
    original_img = img.copy()
    
    # 1. Attempt to use rembg if installed for professional AR background removal
    try:
        # Pre-check onnxruntime to avoid rembg module-level sys.exit(1) execution
        import onnxruntime
        from rembg import remove
        removed = remove(img)
        if removed:
            feathered = apply_edge_feathering(removed, feather_radius=8)
            # Autocrop transparent margins to make the product fill the canvas
            bbox = feathered.getbbox()
            if bbox:
                logger.info("Successfully removed showroom background using rembg library.")
                return feathered.crop(bbox)
            else:
                logger.warning("rembg produced a completely transparent image. Discarding and falling back to chroma keying.")
    except BaseException as rembg_err:
        logger.warning("rembg failed, not installed, or exited: %s. Falling back to corner-sample keying.", str(rembg_err))

    # 2. Fallback: Corner-sample chroma keying (works on gray/off-white studio backdrops)
    img = img.convert("RGBA")
    width, height = img.size
    pixels = img.load()
    
    # Sample the four corners
    corners = [
        pixels[0, 0],
        pixels[width - 1, 0],
        pixels[0, height - 1],
        pixels[width - 1, height - 1]
    ]
    
    # Calculate average corner color (RGB)
    avg_r = sum(c[0] for c in corners) // 4
    avg_g = sum(c[1] for c in corners) // 4
    avg_b = sum(c[2] for c in corners) // 4
    
    # Standard deviation threshold check to verify it's a solid backdrop
    import math
    dists = [math.sqrt((c[0]-avg_r)**2 + (c[1]-avg_g)**2 + (c[2]-avg_b)**2) for c in corners]
    is_solid_backdrop = all(d < 15 for d in dists) # corners are highly similar
    
    new_data = []
    datas = img.getdata()
    
    # Define match threshold
    threshold = 32
    
    for item in datas:
        r, g, b, a = item
        
        # Check distance to average corner color
        dist = math.sqrt((r - avg_r)**2 + (g - avg_g)**2 + (b - avg_b)**2)
        
        # Key out near-white only if the backdrop average corner is near-white
        if (avg_r > 230 and avg_g > 230 and avg_b > 230) and (r > 248 and g > 248 and b > 248):
            new_data.append((r, g, b, 0))
        elif is_solid_backdrop and dist < threshold:
            new_data.append((r, g, b, 0))
        else:
            new_data.append(item)
            
    img.putdata(new_data)
    feathered = apply_edge_feathering(img, feather_radius=12)
    # Autocrop transparent margins to make the product fill the canvas
    bbox = feathered.getbbox()
    if bbox:
        return feathered.crop(bbox)
        
    logger.warning("All background removal attempts resulted in an empty image. Returning original image.")
    return original_img


class ReconstructionAgent:
    """Production-grade ReconstructionAgent responsible for room layout delta generation."""

    def __init__(self, provider: str = "huggingface") -> None:
        """Initializes the ReconstructionAgent."""
        self.provider_name = provider
        self.inpaint_pipe = None
        self.product_image_cache = {}
        from models.text_model import TextModel
        from models.vision_model import VisionModel
        self.text_model = TextModel(provider=provider)
        self.vision_model = VisionModel(provider=provider)
        logger.info("ReconstructionAgent initialized")

    PLACEMENT_CONFIG = {
        "normal": {
            "bed_side_clearance": 100.0,
            "sofa_clearance": 60.0,
            "chair_operating_clearance": 75.0,
            "desk_clearance": 75.0,
            "bookcase_clearance": 60.0,
            "tv_unit_clearance": 60.0,
        },
        "compact": {
            "bed_side_clearance": 60.0,
            "sofa_clearance": 45.0,
            "chair_operating_clearance": 60.0,
            "desk_clearance": 50.0,
            "bookcase_clearance": 40.0,
            "tv_unit_clearance": 40.0,
        },
        "ultra_compact": {
            "bed_side_clearance": 30.0,
            "sofa_clearance": 30.0,
            "chair_operating_clearance": 40.0,
            "desk_clearance": 40.0,
            "bookcase_clearance": 30.0,
            "tv_unit_clearance": 30.0,
        },
        "micro": {
            "bed_side_clearance": 15.0,
            "sofa_clearance": 15.0,
            "chair_operating_clearance": 20.0,
            "desk_clearance": 25.0,
            "bookcase_clearance": 15.0,
            "tv_unit_clearance": 15.0,
        }
    }

    @staticmethod
    def classify_product(item: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        # Check if conformed fields already exist (backward compatibility)
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            p_domain = item.get("placement_domain")
            p_cat = item.get("canonical_category")
            if p_domain and p_cat:
                domain = p_domain
            else:
                from utils.product_classifier import ProductClassifier
                _, domain = ProductClassifier.classify(item)
        else:
            domain = "floor"
            
        # Map classifier domains to conformed local reconstruction domains
        if domain == "ceiling":
            return "ceiling"
        elif domain in ["support_surface", "surface", "ceiling_or_surface"]:
            return "surface"
        elif domain in ["wall", "wall_or_surface"]:
            return "wall"
        return "floor"

    @staticmethod
    def normalize_product_dimensions(item: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        # Priority order:
        # 1. Exact catalog dims in item["dimensions"]
        # 2. Specs in metadata or other keys
        # 3. Regex on title
        # 4. Fallback category defaults
        
        dims = item.get("dimensions") or {}
        w = float(dims.get("width_cm") or item.get("width_cm") or 0.0)
        d = float(dims.get("depth_cm") or item.get("depth_cm") or 0.0)
        h = float(dims.get("height_cm") or item.get("height_cm") or 0.0)
        
        source = "verified" if w > 0 else None
        confidence = 1.0 if source == "verified" else 0.55
        
        title_lower = str(item.get("title", item.get("name", ""))).lower()
        
        if not source:
            # Try to match specs in item metadata
            meta = item.get("metadata") or {}
            if "width" in meta or "depth" in meta or "height" in meta:
                w = float(meta.get("width") or meta.get("width_cm") or 0.0)
                d = float(meta.get("depth") or meta.get("depth_cm") or 0.0)
                h = float(meta.get("height") or meta.get("height_cm") or 0.0)
                if w > 0:
                    source = "metadata"
                    confidence = 0.90
        
        if not source:
            # Check title regex patterns
            import re
            # Check for patterns like "120x60x75" or "80x30x180"
            cross_match = re.search(r'(\d+)\s*x\s*(\d+)(?:\s*x\s*(\d+))?\s*(?:cm)?', title_lower)
            if cross_match:
                w = float(cross_match.group(1))
                d = float(cross_match.group(2))
                if cross_match.group(3):
                    h = float(cross_match.group(3))
                else:
                    h = 60.0
                source = "title_regex_cross"
                confidence = 0.80
            else:
                # Check for inch pattern like "52 in." or "52 inch" or "52\""
                inch_match = re.search(r'(\d+)\s*(?:in|inch|”|")', title_lower)
                if inch_match:
                    val_cm = float(inch_match.group(1)) * 2.54
                    w = val_cm
                    d = val_cm
                    h = 30.0 # ceiling fan height or tv unit
                    source = "title_regex_inch"
                    confidence = 0.80
                    
        if not source:
            source = "category_estimate"
            confidence = 0.55
            category = str(item.get("category", "")).lower()
            if "desk" in category or "table" in category:
                w, d, h = 100.0, 60.0, 75.0
            elif "chair" in category or "seating" in category:
                w, d, h = 60.0, 60.0, 85.0
            elif "bookshelf" in category or "shelf" in category or "bookcase" in title_lower:
                w, d, h = 80.0, 30.0, 180.0
            elif "tv unit" in title_lower or "tv stand" in title_lower or "television" in category:
