Tables

----------------------------------------------------
VERY SMALL OBJECTS
----------------------------------------------------
Charging cables
Loose wires
Connectors
Switches
Sockets
Plug points
Adapters
Small decorations
Wall hooks
Door handles

----------------------------------------------------
OBJECT DETECTION RULES
----------------------------------------------------
1. Detect every visible object regardless of importance.
2. Do not ignore objects simply because they are small.
3. If an object occupies only a small part of the image but remains recognizable, include it.
4. Prefer over-detection rather than under-detection.
5. If uncertain, include the object with lower confidence.
6. Use confidence scores instead of omitting uncertain objects.
7. Detect partially visible objects.
8. Detect occluded objects if enough evidence exists.
9. Detect decorative objects.
10. Detect electronics and accessories.
11. Detect cables and power equipment whenever visible.
12. Detect wall-mounted items.
13. Detect ceiling-mounted items.
14. Detect floor-level items.
15. Detect objects placed on furniture surfaces.
16. Detect objects attached to walls.
17. Detect objects inside shelves and racks if visible.

----------------------------------------------------
MULTI-VIEW RULES
----------------------------------------------------
Multiple images belong to the same room.
Merge observations from all images.
If an object appears in multiple images:
- preserve highest confidence attributes
- preserve best dimensions
- preserve best bounding box
- preserve best color estimate
Never create duplicates.

----------------------------------------------------
FULL IMAGE ANALYSIS
----------------------------------------------------
First perform global scene understanding:
- room type
- room dimensions
- major furniture
- structural elements
- style
- free space
- accessibility

----------------------------------------------------
TILE ANALYSIS
----------------------------------------------------
Then inspect local regions independently:
Top Left
Top Right
Bottom Left
Bottom Right
Center Region

For each region:
- inspect shelves
- inspect desk surfaces
- inspect table surfaces
- inspect windowsills
- inspect floor areas
- inspect wall regions
- inspect corners
Detect all local objects.

----------------------------------------------------
SURFACE ANALYSIS
----------------------------------------------------
Inspect:
- desk surface
- bedside table
- dining table
- shelves
- TV unit
- cabinets
- floor near furniture
Detect objects resting on surfaces.

----------------------------------------------------
ADAPTIVE ZOOM ANALYSIS
----------------------------------------------------
If the following objects are detected:
desk
table
bookshelf
TV unit
cabinet
bedside table
perform secondary inspection of that region.

Examples:
Desk
→ laptop
→ keyboard
→ mouse
→ charger
→ lamp
→ notebook
→ bottle

Bookshelf
→ books
→ decor
→ plants
→ frames

TV Unit
→ remote
→ speakers
→ console
→ set-top box

Bedside Table
→ lamp
→ phone
→ charger
→ water bottle

----------------------------------------------------
CONFIDENCE POLICY
----------------------------------------------------
Major Furniture: minimum confidence = 0.60
Medium Objects: minimum confidence = 0.45
Small Objects: minimum confidence = 0.30
Very Small Objects: minimum confidence = 0.20
Never discard low confidence small objects.

----------------------------------------------------
OUTPUT REQUIREMENTS
----------------------------------------------------
Return ONLY valid JSON.
Do not hallucinate objects that are not visible.
Prefer low confidence detections instead of omission.
Generate an exhaustive room inventory.
The goal is maximum recall rather than maximum precision.
Missing objects are worse than false positives.

Format the response exactly as a JSON object matching this schema:

## File: `base_provider.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/providers/base_provider.py`

```python
"""Base Provider Module for VisionSpace AI.

Defines the contract interface that all backend model execution providers must implement.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union, Generator

class BaseProvider:
    """Interface class that all model providers must implement."""

    def load(self, model_name: str, task: str, config_kwargs: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        """Loads model weights, tokenizers, and configuration parameters.

        Args:
            model_name: The name/identifier of the model on the backend.
            task: Task key (e.g. 'vision', 'reasoning').
            config_kwargs: Key-value dictionary of additional parameters.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def unload(self) -> None:
        """Unloads model components from device to free hardware resources."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def generate(
        self,
        prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        images: Optional[List[Any]] = None,
        messages: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        system_prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs: Any
    ) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], Generator[str, None, None]]:
        """Generates text completion based on prompt instructions.

        Returns:
            Dictionary containing structured output fields or streaming generator.
        """
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def gpu_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Gathers properties of active GPU hardware."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def get_memory_usage(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Gets diagnostics detailing GPU memory utilization."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def get_model_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Gathers config settings and capabilities of active model."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def get_processor_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns processor class name and tokenizer vocabulary properties."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def stop_generation(self) -> None:
        """Signals active generation thread to halt execution."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def count_tokens(self, text: Union[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> int:
        """Returns the tokens count of a text string or list of messages."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def estimate_cost(self, tokens: int, mode: str = "input") -> float:
        """Estimates model query prices based on token counts."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def remaining_context(self, current_tokens: int) -> int:
        """Returns remaining headroom tokens count."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def list_cached_models(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Lists downloaded model repositories stored in the Cache."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def clear_cache(self) -> bool:
        """Clears local Cache directories."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

    def cache_size(self) -> float:
        """Gets total disk storage occupied by the Cache in GB."""
        raise NotImplementedError()

```

## File: `deepinfra_provider.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/providers/deepinfra_provider.py`

```python
"""DeepInfra Provider Module for VisionSpace AI.

Integrates DeepInfra's OpenAI-compatible endpoint to execute cheap and fast completions
for Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct and other text models.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import io
import json
import logging
import os
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional, Union, Generator

import requests
from PIL import Image

import config
from providers.base_provider import BaseProvider

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.DeepInfraProvider")

class DeepInfraProvider(BaseProvider):
    """DeepInfra/Vercel AI Gateway API provider implementing the BaseProvider contract interface."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """Initializes the DeepInfraProvider."""
        self.vercel_key = os.environ.get("VERCEL_API_KEY") or getattr(config, "VERCEL_API_KEY", None)
        self.deepinfra_key = os.environ.get("DEEPINFRA_API_KEY") or getattr(config, "DEEPINFRA_API_KEY", None)
        self.api_key = self.vercel_key or self.deepinfra_key
        
        if self.vercel_key:
            self.model_name = "deepinfra/alibaba/qwen3-vl-instruct"
            self.base_url = "https://ai-gateway.vercel.sh/v1"
        else:
            self.model_name = getattr(config, "MODEL", "Qwen/Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct")
            self.base_url = "https://api.deepinfra.com/v1/openai"

        self.task = "reasoning"
        self._stopped = False
        self.loaded = False

        if not self.api_key:
            logger.warning("API keys (DEEPINFRA_API_KEY / VERCEL_API_KEY) are missing. Running in mock fallback mode.")

    def load(self, model_name: str, task: str, config_kwargs: Optional[Dict[str, Any]] = None) -> None:
        """Saves config metadata. No local weights loading required for API provider."""
        self.vercel_key = os.environ.get("VERCEL_API_KEY") or getattr(config, "VERCEL_API_KEY", None)
        self.deepinfra_key = os.environ.get("DEEPINFRA_API_KEY") or getattr(config, "DEEPINFRA_API_KEY", None)
        self.api_key = self.vercel_key or self.deepinfra_key

        if self.vercel_key:
            self.model_name = "deepinfra/alibaba/qwen3-vl-instruct"
            self.base_url = "https://ai-gateway.vercel.sh/v1"
        else:
            self.model_name = model_name
            self.base_url = "https://api.deepinfra.com/v1/openai"

        self.task = task
        self.loaded = True
        logger.info("API provider initialized for model '%s' via '%s' (task: %s)", self.model_name, self.base_url, task)

    def unload(self) -> None:
        """No GPU memory cleanup needed for API-based provider."""
        self.loaded = False

    def _pil_to_base64(self, img: Image.Image) -> str:
        """Encodes PIL image as base64 string."""
        try:
            buffered = io.BytesIO()
            img.convert("RGB").save(buffered, format="JPEG")
            return base64.b64encode(buffered.getvalue()).decode("utf-8")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error("Failed to encode PIL Image to Base64: %s", str(e))
            raise

    def generate(
        self,
        prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        images: Optional[List[Any]] = None,
        messages: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        system_prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs: Any
    ) -> Union[Dict[str, Any], Generator[str, None, None]]:
        """Invokes chat completions endpoint on DeepInfra. Falls back to mock in development if API key is missing."""
        self._stopped = False
        api_key = self.api_key or os.environ.get("VERCEL_API_KEY") or os.environ.get("DEEPINFRA_API_KEY")
        if not api_key:
            logger.warning("No API credentials configured. Running in DEVELOPMENT MOCK MODE.")
            return self._generate_mock(prompt, images, messages, system_prompt, **kwargs)

        # 1. Format payload messages array
        formatted_messages: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        if messages:
            formatted_messages = list(messages)
        else:
            user_content: Union[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = ""
            if images:
                content_list = []
                if prompt:
                    content_list.append({"type": "text", "text": prompt})
                for img in images:
                    if isinstance(img, Image.Image):
                        b64_str = self._pil_to_base64(img)
                        content_list.append({
                            "type": "image_url",
                            "image_url": {
                                "url": f"data:image/jpeg;base64,{b64_str}"
                            }
                        })
                user_content = content_list
            else:
                user_content = prompt or ""

            formatted_messages = [{"role": "user", "content": user_content}]

        if system_prompt:
            formatted_messages = [{"role": "system", "content": system_prompt}] + formatted_messages

        # 2. Call API completions
        url = f"{self.base_url}/chat/completions"
        headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {api_key}",
            "Content-Type": "application/json"
        }

        payload = {
            "model": self.model_name,
            "messages": formatted_messages,
            "temperature": kwargs.get("temperature", 0.1),
            "max_tokens": kwargs.get("max_new_tokens", 2048),
        }

        if kwargs.get("response_format") == "json" or "json" in str(kwargs.get("response_format", "")).lower():
            payload["response_format"] = {"type": "json_object"}

        logger.info("Sending request to DeepInfra API (%s)...", self.model_name)
        try:
            response = requests.post(url, headers=headers, json=payload, timeout=60)
            if response.status_code != 200:
                logger.warning("DeepInfra API returned status code %d: %s. Falling back to DEVELOPMENT MOCK MODE.", response.status_code, response.text)
                return self._generate_mock(prompt, images, messages, system_prompt, **kwargs)
            res_json = response.json()
            choices = res_json.get("choices", [])
            if not choices:
                logger.warning("DeepInfra API returned empty choices. Falling back to DEVELOPMENT MOCK MODE.")
                return self._generate_mock(prompt, images, messages, system_prompt, **kwargs)
        except Exception as api_err:
            logger.warning("DeepInfra API request failed: %s. Falling back to DEVELOPMENT MOCK MODE.", str(api_err))
            return self._generate_mock(prompt, images, messages, system_prompt, **kwargs)

        choice = choices[0]
        text_out = choice.get("message", {}).get("content", "")

        input_tokens = res_json.get("usage", {}).get("prompt_tokens", 0)
        output_tokens = res_json.get("usage", {}).get("completion_tokens", 0)

        # Standard return dict format
        return {
            "text": text_out,
            "response": text_out,
            "success": True,
            "tokens": {
                "input": input_tokens,
                "output": output_tokens
            }
        }

    def gpu_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """DeepInfra uses cloud compute, so return empty diagnostics."""
        return {"device": "Cloud API (DeepInfra)"}

    def get_memory_usage(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """DeepInfra uses cloud compute, so return empty memory status."""
        return {"allocated_bytes": 0, "reserved_bytes": 0}

    def get_model_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns metadata about the active model."""
        return {
            "model_name": self.model_name,
            "context_length": 32768,
            "supports_images": True
        }

    def get_processor_info(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns tokenizer information."""
        return {"processor": "DeepInfra API Tokenizer"}

    def stop_generation(self) -> None:
        """Sets internal stop flag."""
        self._stopped = True

    def count_tokens(self, text: Union[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]]) -> int:
        """Approximates token count by words length heuristics."""
        if isinstance(text, list):
            flat = ""
            for m in text:
                flat += f"{m.get('role', '')}: {m.get('content', '')}\n"
            text = flat
        return len(text.split())

    def estimate_cost(self, tokens: int, mode: str = "input") -> float:
        """Calculates estimated price based on $0.20/M input and $0.88/M output pricing."""
        if mode == "input":
            return (tokens / 1_000_000.0) * 0.20
        else:
            return (tokens / 1_000_000.0) * 0.88

    def remaining_context(self, current_tokens: int) -> int:
        """Returns context limits headroom."""
        return max(0, 32768 - current_tokens)

    def list_cached_models(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """DeepInfra doesn't cache local repository directories."""
        return []

    def clear_cache(self) -> bool:
        """DeepInfra doesn't cache local files."""
        return True

    def cache_size(self) -> float:
        """Return zero cache footprint."""
        return 0.0

    def _generate_mock(
        self,
        prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        images: Optional[List[Any]] = None,
        messages: Optional[List[Dict[str, Any]]] = None,
        system_prompt: Optional[str] = None,
        **kwargs: Any
    ) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Returns mock responses conforming to V2 schemas for development and local testing."""
        prompt_str = str(prompt or "")
        if messages:
            prompt_str += " " + " ".join(str(m.get("content", "")) for m in messages)

        mock_data = {}
        if "yes or no" in prompt_str.lower():
            return {
                "text": "YES",
                "response": "YES",
                "success": True,
                "tokens": {"input": 100, "output": 100}
            }
        
        if self.task == "vision":
            mock_data = {
              "room_type": "bedroom",
              "objects": [
                {
                  "id": "bed_001",
                  "name": "bed",
                  "category": "furniture",
                  "estimated_dimensions": {"width_cm": 180, "depth_cm": 200, "height_cm": 90},
                  "room_position": {"x_cm": 200, "y_cm": 150, "z_cm": 45},
                  "rotation": 0
                },
                {
                  "id": "wardrobe_001",
                  "name": "wardrobe",
                  "category": "furniture",
                  "estimated_dimensions": {"width_cm": 120, "depth_cm": 60, "height_cm": 210},
                  "room_position": {"x_cm": 380, "y_cm": 80, "z_cm": 105},
                  "rotation": 90
                }
              ],
              "doors": [
                {
                  "id": "door_001",
                  "type": "swing",
                  "estimated_dimensions": {"width_cm": 90, "depth_cm": 10, "height_cm": 200},
                  "room_position": {"x_cm": 50, "y_cm": 350, "z_cm": 100}
                }
              ],
              "windows": [
                {
                  "id": "window_001",
                  "type": "standard",
                  "estimated_dimensions": {"width_cm": 120, "depth_cm": 20, "height_cm": 150},
                  "room_position": {"x_cm": 240, "y_cm": 50, "z_cm": 120}
                }
              ],
              "walls": [
                {"orientation": "north", "color": "warm white", "material": "plaster"},
                {"orientation": "south", "color": "warm white", "material": "plaster"},
                {"orientation": "east", "color": "warm white", "material": "plaster"},
                {"orientation": "west", "color": "warm white", "material": "plaster"}
              ],
              "floor": {
                "material": "oak wood",
                "color": "natural brown"
              },
              "ceiling": {
                "height_cm": 290,
                "color": "white"
              },
              "confidence_score": 0.92
            }
        elif self.task == "spatial" or "spatial" in prompt_str.lower():
            mock_data = {
              "walkability_score": 88,
              "clearance_issues": ["No major clearance blocks detected."],
              "unused_space": ["corners"],
              "collision_objects": [],
              "usable_area_percentage": 80
            }
        elif self.task == "design" or "design" in prompt_str.lower():
            mock_data = {
              "design_theme": "modern minimal",
              "wall_color": "warm white",
              "floor_material": "oak wood",
              "lighting_type": "warm lighting",
              "dominant_colors": ["#ffffff", "#c49a6c"],
              "style_score": 0.91
            }
        elif self.task == "budget":
            mock_data = {
              "subtotal": 28500,
              "shipping": 1200,
              "tax": 3100,
              "total": 32800,
              "budget_limit": 35000,
              "remaining_budget": 2200
            }
        elif self.task == "explain" or "explain" in prompt_str.lower():
            mock_data = {
              "product_explanations": ["Selected study desk because it fits clearance bounds.", "Selected chair because it provides ergonomic support."],
              "placement_explanations": ["Placed desk near the window for natural light.", "Placed chair under the desk to maximize walking space."],
              "budget_explanations": ["Selected products are within the limit of Rs. 40000."]
            }
        else:
            mock_data = {"status": "success", "message": "Development mock response"}

        import json
        return {
            "text": json.dumps(mock_data),
            "response": json.dumps(mock_data),
            "success": True,
            "tokens": {"input": 100, "output": 100}
        }
```
