```python
# utils/object_enricher.py
"""
VisionSpace AI — Production Object Enricher
============================================
Transforms raw Vision Agent detections into fully-enriched design objects
by delegating to the Reasoning Model (ModelManager) rather than a static
lookup table.

Key design decisions:
  - Model-agnostic: uses ModelManager.generate(), so any registered
    reasoning model can be swapped in.
  - Fallback chain: LLM → rule-based heuristics → raw detection output.
  - Batch enrichment: enriches multiple objects in one call to reduce
    round-trips.
  - Caching: enriched objects are cached by (label, colour, material, style)
    key to avoid redundant inference on repeated detections.

Usage::

    enricher = ObjectEnricher()
    enriched = enricher.enrich(objects, room_type="bedroom", style="japandi")
    # each obj now has: style_compatibility, keep_or_replace, accessory_pairings, …
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import logging
import time
from typing import Any

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Rule-Based Fallbacks (no LLM needed)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_STYLE_COMPATIBILITY: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "sofa":           ["modern", "scandinavian", "mid_century_modern", "industrial"],
    "armchair":       ["traditional", "mid_century_modern", "bohemian", "maximalist"],
    "coffee_table":   ["modern", "contemporary", "scandinavian", "industrial"],
    "bookshelf":      ["scandinavian", "industrial", "mid_century_modern", "bohemian"],
    "dining_table":   ["modern", "farmhouse", "rustic", "contemporary"],
    "bed":            ["modern", "scandinavian", "japandi", "traditional"],
    "wardrobe":       ["modern", "contemporary", "minimalist"],
    "floor_lamp":     ["mid_century_modern", "art_deco", "modern", "industrial"],
    "rug":            ["bohemian", "traditional", "scandinavian", "modern"],
    "plant":          ["bohemian", "japandi", "coastal", "scandinavian"],
    "mirror":         ["art_deco", "modern", "hollywood_regency", "traditional"],
    "curtain":        ["traditional", "farmhouse", "coastal", "bohemian"],
}

_MATERIAL_TIER: dict[str, str] = {
    "solid wood":   "premium",
    "oak":          "premium",
    "walnut":       "premium",
    "teak":         "premium",
    "marble":       "luxury",
    "brass":        "luxury",
    "velvet":       "premium",
    "leather":      "premium",
    "mdf":          "budget",
    "particle board": "budget",
    "plywood":      "mid",
    "pine":         "mid",
    "fabric":       "mid",
    "linen":        "mid",
    "polyester":    "budget",
    "plastic":      "budget",
    "metal":        "mid",
    "glass":        "mid",
    "rattan":       "mid",
    "bamboo":       "mid",
}

_KEEP_REPLACE_RULES: dict[str, dict] = {
    # (condition, material_tier) → recommendation
    ("good",   "premium"): {"decision": "keep",      "priority": 5},
    ("good",   "luxury"):  {"decision": "keep",      "priority": 5},
    ("good",   "mid"):     {"decision": "keep",      "priority": 4},
    ("good",   "budget"):  {"decision": "replace",   "priority": 2},
    ("worn",   "premium"): {"decision": "refinish",  "priority": 3},
    ("worn",   "mid"):     {"decision": "replace",   "priority": 2},
    ("worn",   "budget"):  {"decision": "replace",   "priority": 1},
    ("damaged","premium"): {"decision": "refinish",  "priority": 3},
    ("damaged","mid"):     {"decision": "replace",   "priority": 1},
    ("damaged","budget"):  {"decision": "replace",   "priority": 1},
}

_TYPICAL_DIMENSIONS: dict[str, dict[str, int]] = {
    "sofa":         {"width_cm": 220, "depth_cm": 90,  "height_cm": 85},
    "armchair":     {"width_cm": 80,  "depth_cm": 80,  "height_cm": 90},
    "coffee_table": {"width_cm": 120, "depth_cm": 60,  "height_cm": 45},
    "dining_table": {"width_cm": 180, "depth_cm": 90,  "height_cm": 75},
    "dining_chair": {"width_cm": 45,  "depth_cm": 50,  "height_cm": 90},
    "bookshelf":    {"width_cm": 80,  "depth_cm": 30,  "height_cm": 180},
    "wardrobe":     {"width_cm": 120, "depth_cm": 60,  "height_cm": 200},
    "bed":          {"width_cm": 160, "depth_cm": 200, "height_cm": 50},
    "desk":         {"width_cm": 140, "depth_cm": 70,  "height_cm": 75},
    "floor_lamp":   {"width_cm": 40,  "depth_cm": 40,  "height_cm": 160},
    "rug":          {"width_cm": 200, "depth_cm": 300, "height_cm": 2},
    "tv_unit":      {"width_cm": 150, "depth_cm": 45,  "height_cm": 50},
}

_ACCESSORY_PAIRINGS: dict[str, list[str]] = {
    "sofa":         ["throw pillow", "throw blanket", "side table", "floor lamp", "rug"],
    "armchair":     ["side table", "floor lamp", "ottoman", "throw pillow"],
    "dining_table": ["table runner", "centrepiece", "candle holders", "placemats"],
    "bed":          ["bedside lamp", "nightstand", "throw blanket", "area rug"],
    "bookshelf":    ["decorative books", "small plant", "framed photo", "decorative object"],
    "desk":         ["desk lamp", "monitor riser", "plant", "pen holder", "art print"],
    "coffee_table": ["coffee table books", "decorative tray", "candle", "small plant"],
    "floor_lamp":   ["side table", "armchair", "area rug"],
}


class ObjectEnricher:
    """Enriches detected objects with design metadata.

    Attempts LLM-based enrichment via ModelManager when available,
    falling back to rule-based heuristics automatically.

    Args:
        use_llm: Whether to attempt LLM enrichment (default True).
                 Set to False to force rule-based mode (faster, no GPU needed).
        cache_ttl_seconds: How long to cache enrichment results (default 1 hour).
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        use_llm: bool = True,
        cache_ttl_seconds: int = 3600,
    ) -> None:
        self._use_llm = use_llm
        self._cache_ttl = cache_ttl_seconds
        self._cache: dict[str, tuple[dict, float]] = {}   # key → (result, expiry)
        self._manager = None  # Lazy import to avoid circular deps

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Public API
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def enrich(
        self,
        objects: list[dict],
        room_type: str = "living_room",
        style: str = "modern",
    ) -> list[dict]:
        """Enrich a list of detected objects with design metadata.

        Args:
            objects:   List of detection dicts (each has ``label``, ``colour``,
                       ``material``, ``condition``, ``bbox``).
            room_type: Room context for enrichment.
            style:     Target design style.

        Returns:
            List of enriched object dicts with additional fields.
        """
        enriched = []
        for obj in objects:
            try:
                result = self._enrich_single(obj, room_type=room_type, style=style)
                enriched.append(result)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning(
                    "Object enrichment failed for '%s': %s — using raw detection.",
                    obj.get("label", "?"), exc,
                )
                enriched.append({**obj, "_enrichment_status": "failed"})
        return enriched

    def enrich_single(
        self,
        obj: dict,
        room_type: str = "living_room",
        style: str = "modern",
    ) -> dict:
        """Enrich a single detected object. Public wrapper for :meth:`_enrich_single`."""
        return self._enrich_single(obj, room_type=room_type, style=style)

    def clear_cache(self) -> int:
        """Clear the enrichment result cache.

        Returns:
            Number of entries cleared.
        """
        count = len(self._cache)
        self._cache.clear()
        logger.info("ObjectEnricher cache cleared (%d entries)", count)
        return count

    def cache_stats(self) -> dict:
        """Return cache statistics."""
        now = time.monotonic()
        alive = sum(1 for _, (_, exp) in self._cache.items() if exp > now)
        return {
            "total_entries": len(self._cache),
            "alive_entries": alive,
            "expired_entries": len(self._cache) - alive,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Internal Enrichment
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _enrich_single(
        self,
        obj: dict,
        room_type: str,
        style: str,
    ) -> dict:
        label    = obj.get("label", "object").lower().strip()
        colour   = obj.get("colour", "unknown")
        material = obj.get("material", "unknown")
        condition = obj.get("condition", "good")

        cache_key = self._cache_key(label, colour, material, style)
        cached = self._get_cached(cache_key)
        if cached is not None:
            return {**obj, **cached, "_enrichment_source": "cache"}

        # Try LLM enrichment first
        if self._use_llm:
            llm_result = self._enrich_via_llm(
                label, colour, material, condition, room_type, style
            )
            if llm_result:
                self._set_cache(cache_key, llm_result)
                return {**obj, **llm_result, "_enrichment_source": "llm"}

        # Fallback: rule-based
        rule_result = self._enrich_via_rules(label, colour, material, condition, style)
        self._set_cache(cache_key, rule_result)
        return {**obj, **rule_result, "_enrichment_source": "rules"}

    def _enrich_via_llm(
        self,
        label: str,
        colour: str,
        material: str,
        condition: str,
        room_type: str,
        style: str,
    ) -> dict | None:
        """Attempt LLM enrichment via ModelManager.  Returns None on any failure."""
        try:
            from models.model_manager import ModelManager
            from utils.prompt_templates import object_enrichment_prompt

            manager = ModelManager.get_instance()
            prompt = object_enrichment_prompt(
                label=label, colour=colour, material=material,
                room_type=room_type, style=style,
            )

            messages = [
                {"role": "system",    "content": prompt["system"]},
                {"role": "user",      "content": prompt["user"]},
            ]
            response = manager.generate(task="reasoning", messages=messages)

            if not response or not response.get("text"):
                return None

            raw_text = response["text"].strip()
            # Strip markdown fences if present
            if raw_text.startswith("```"):
                raw_text = raw_text.split("```")[1]
                if raw_text.startswith("json"):
                    raw_text = raw_text[4:]

            parsed = json.loads(raw_text)
            return parsed.get("enriched_object", parsed)

        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("LLM enrichment skipped: %s", exc)
            return None

    def _enrich_via_rules(
        self,
        label: str,
        colour: str,
        material: str,
        condition: str,
        style: str,
    ) -> dict:
        """Return rule-based enrichment metadata for a single object."""
        # Style compatibility
        style_compat = self._resolve_style_compat(label)

        # Material quality tier
        material_tier = "mid"
        for mat_kw, tier in _MATERIAL_TIER.items():
            if mat_kw in material.lower():
                material_tier = tier
                break

        # Keep / replace decision
        rule = _KEEP_REPLACE_RULES.get(
            (condition, material_tier),
            {"decision": "keep", "priority": 3},
        )

        # Typical dimensions
        dims = _TYPICAL_DIMENSIONS.get(label, {"width_cm": 0, "depth_cm": 0, "height_cm": 0})

        # Accessories
        accessories = _ACCESSORY_PAIRINGS.get(label, [])

        # Style compatibility score (1 = match, 0 = mismatch)
        style_match = style in style_compat

        return {
            "style_compatibility":    style_compat,
            "style_match":            style_match,
            "typical_dimensions":     dims,
            "material_quality":       material_tier,
            "age_estimate":           "unknown",
            "replacement_priority":   rule["priority"],
            "keep_or_replace":        rule["decision"],
            "reason": (
                f"{label.capitalize()} in {condition} condition with {material_tier}-quality "
                f"materials → {rule['decision']}."
            ),
            "upgrade_options":        [],
            "accessory_pairings":     accessories,
        }

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Style Compatibility Resolution
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _resolve_style_compat(self, label: str) -> list[str]:
        """Return compatible styles for a label (exact or fuzzy match)."""
        # Exact match
        if label in _STYLE_COMPATIBILITY:
            return _STYLE_COMPATIBILITY[label]
        # Fuzzy: check if label contains a known key
        for key, styles in _STYLE_COMPATIBILITY.items():
            if key in label:
                return styles
        # Generic fallback
        return ["modern", "contemporary", "transitional"]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Cache Helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------

    @staticmethod
    def _cache_key(label: str, colour: str, material: str, style: str) -> str:
        raw = f"{label}|{colour}|{material}|{style}"
        return hashlib.md5(raw.encode()).hexdigest()

    def _get_cached(self, key: str) -> dict | None:
        entry = self._cache.get(key)
        if entry is None:
            return None
        result, expiry = entry
        if time.monotonic() > expiry:
            del self._cache[key]
            return None
        return result

    def _set_cache(self, key: str, value: dict) -> None:
        self._cache[key] = (value, time.monotonic() + self._cache_ttl)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Module-Level Convenience Functions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_default_enricher: ObjectEnricher | None = None


def get_enricher(use_llm: bool = True) -> ObjectEnricher:
    """Return the module-level singleton ObjectEnricher."""
    global _default_enricher
    if _default_enricher is None:
        _default_enricher = ObjectEnricher(use_llm=use_llm)
    return _default_enricher


def enrich_objects(
    objects: list[dict],
    room_type: str = "living_room",
    style: str = "modern",
    use_llm: bool = True,
) -> list[dict]:
    """Module-level convenience: enrich a list of detected objects.

    Args:
        objects:   Detected object list from Vision Agent.
        room_type: Room context.
        style:     Target design style.
        use_llm:   Whether to use LLM enrichment (fallback to rules if False).

    Returns:
        Enriched object list.
    """
    return get_enricher(use_llm=use_llm).enrich(
        objects, room_type=room_type, style=style
    )


__all__ = [
    "ObjectEnricher",
    "get_enricher",
    "enrich_objects",
]

## File: `object_fusion.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/utils/object_fusion.py`

```python
# utils/object_fusion.py
import logging
from collections import defaultdict
from typing import Any, Dict, List

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class ObjectFusion:
    """Handles semantic object deduplication and multi-view attribute fusion."""

    def __init__(self, nms_threshold: float = 0.45) -> None:
        self.nms_threshold = nms_threshold
        # Semantic classes that typically exist as a single unique instance in a room
        self.unique_singletons = {"bed", "desk", "dresser", "wardrobe", "sofa"}
        self.compatibility_groups = [
            {"bed", "mattress", "cot", "crib", "daybed"},
            {"chair", "sofa", "armchair", "stool", "couch", "seating", "bench", "recliner"},
            {"desk", "table", "nightstand", "counter", "desk_table"},
            {"dresser", "wardrobe", "closet", "cabinet", "cupboard", "drawer", "chest", "shelving", "bookcase"},
            {"lamp", "light", "sconce", "lighting", "chandelier", "fixture"}
        ]

    def are_compatible(self, label1: str, label2: str) -> bool:
        if label1 == label2:
            return True
        for group in self.compatibility_groups:
            if label1 in group and label2 in group:
                return True
                
        return False

    def normalize_label(self, obj: Dict[str, Any]) -> str:
        """Helper to extract a clean semantic label from object fields."""
        name = (obj.get("name") or "").lower()
        category = (obj.get("category") or "").lower()
        subcategory = (obj.get("subcategory") or "").lower()
        label = (obj.get("label") or "").lower()
        
        combined = f"{name} {category} {subcategory} {label}"
        if "bed" in combined:
            return "bed"
        if "chair" in combined or "sofa" in combined or "seating" in combined:
            return "chair"
        if "desk" in combined or "table" in combined:
            return "desk"
        if "dresser" in combined or "wardrobe" in combined or "cabinet" in combined or "cupboard" in combined or "drawer" in combined:
            return "dresser"
        if "lamp" in combined or "light" in combined or "sconce" in combined or "lighting" in combined:
            return "lamp"
        if "door" in combined:
            return "door"
        if "window" in combined:
            return "window"
        if "plant" in combined or "flower" in combined:
            return "plant"
        if "painting" in combined or "frame" in combined or "art" in combined or "decor" in combined:
            return "decor"
            
        for val in (name, category, label):
            if val:
                return val.strip().replace(" ", "_")
        return "object"

    def fuse_objects(self, objects: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Deduplicates objects globally across viewpoints using physical and semantic compatibility."""
        if not objects:
            return []

        no_bbox = []
        labeled_objs = []
        for obj in objects:
            label = self.normalize_label(obj)
            obj_copy = dict(obj)
            obj_copy["_normalized_label"] = label
            if "bbox" not in obj_copy:
                no_bbox.append(obj_copy)
            else:
                labeled_objs.append(obj_copy)

        # Group by viewpoint index
        by_view = defaultdict(list)
        for obj in labeled_objs:
            by_view[obj.get("_view_source", 0)].append(obj)

        view_ids = sorted(by_view.keys())
        if not view_ids:
            return objects

        # Initialize global fusion list with the first viewpoint's candidates
        fused_list: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for obj in by_view[view_ids[0]]:
            obj_copy = dict(obj)
            obj_copy["seen_in_views"] = [view_ids[0]]
            obj_copy["_match_scores"] = []
            fused_list.append(obj_copy)

        # Greedily fuse objects from subsequent viewpoint lists
        for v_idx in view_ids[1:]:
            view_objs = by_view[v_idx]
            for obj in view_objs:
                best_match_idx = -1
                best_score = -1.0
                label_o = obj["_normalized_label"]
                
                for k_idx, k_obj in enumerate(fused_list):
                    # Prevent merging candidates from the same viewpoint
                    if v_idx in k_obj.get("seen_in_views", []):
                        continue
                    
                    label_k = k_obj["_normalized_label"]
                    if not self.are_compatible(label_k, label_o):
                        continue # Skip completely incompatible categories
                    
                    score = 0.0
                    
                    # 1. Category Compatibility check
                    if label_k == label_o:
                        score += 0.35
                    else:
                        score += 0.25
 
                    # 2. Word intersection check
                    name_k = k_obj.get("name", "").lower()
                    name_o = obj.get("name", "").lower()
                    words_k = set(name_k.split())
                    words_o = set(name_o.split())
                    if name_k == name_o and name_k:
                        score += 0.25
                    elif words_k.intersection(words_o) and name_k:
                        score += 0.15
 
                    # 3. Material and color matching (mismatch penalizes strongly)
                    mat_k = k_obj.get("material")
                    mat_o = obj.get("material")
                    col_k = k_obj.get("color")
                    col_o = obj.get("color")
                    
                    if mat_k and mat_o:
                        if mat_k.lower() == mat_o.lower():
                            score += 0.2
                        else:
                            score -= 0.45
                    if col_k and col_o:
                        if col_k.lower() == col_o.lower():
                            score += 0.2
                        else:
                            score -= 0.45
                    
                    # 4. Bounding box scale verification (prevent matching small background objects to large foreground ones)
                    w_k = k_obj.get("bbox", {}).get("width", 0)
                    h_k = k_obj.get("bbox", {}).get("height", 0)
                    w_o = obj.get("bbox", {}).get("width", 0)
                    h_o = obj.get("bbox", {}).get("height", 0)
                    if w_k > 0 and h_k > 0 and w_o > 0 and h_o > 0:
                        w_ratio = min(w_k, w_o) / max(w_k, w_o)
                        h_ratio = min(h_k, h_o) / max(h_k, h_o)
                        if w_ratio < 0.3 or h_ratio < 0.3:
                            score -= 0.4 # Severe scale mismatch penalty
                    
                    # 5. Proximity matching (if 3D coordinates are already resolved)
                    pos_k = k_obj.get("position")
                    pos_o = obj.get("position")
                    if pos_k and pos_o:
                        dist = ((pos_k["x_cm"] - pos_o["x_cm"])**2 + (pos_k["y_cm"] - pos_o["y_cm"])**2)**0.5
                        if dist < 120:
                            score += 0.5
                        elif dist < 180:
                            score += 0.35
                        elif dist > 250:
                            score -= 0.6
                        
                    if score > best_score:
                        best_score = score
                        best_match_idx = k_idx
                
                # Use a higher threshold of 0.6 for conservative, precise matches
                if best_score >= 0.6 and best_match_idx != -1:
                    fused_list[best_match_idx] = self.merge_pair(fused_list[best_match_idx], obj)
                    fused_list[best_match_idx]["seen_in_views"].append(v_idx)
                    fused_list[best_match_idx]["_match_scores"].append(best_score)
                else:
                    obj_copy = dict(obj)
                    obj_copy["seen_in_views"] = [v_idx]
                    obj_copy["_match_scores"] = []
                    fused_list.append(obj_copy)

        # Singleton consolidation pass (for beds, dressers, wardrobes, desks, sofas)
        final_list: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        skip_indices = set()
        for i in range(len(fused_list)):
            if i in skip_indices:
                continue
            
            obj1 = fused_list[i]
            label1 = obj1["_normalized_label"]
            
            if label1 in self.unique_singletons:
                # Find all other instances of this singleton class and merge them only if attributes align
                for j in range(i + 1, len(fused_list)):
                    if j in skip_indices:
                        continue
                    obj2 = fused_list[j]
                    label2 = obj2["_normalized_label"]
                    
                    if label2 == label1 or self.are_compatible(label1, label2):
                        col1 = obj1.get("color")
                        col2 = obj2.get("color")
                        mat1 = obj1.get("material")
                        mat2 = obj2.get("material")
                        
                        # If colors or materials mismatch, keep them as distinct objects
                        if col1 and col2 and col1.lower() != col2.lower():
                            continue
                        if mat1 and mat2 and mat1.lower() != mat2.lower():
                            continue
                            
                        obj1 = self.merge_pair(obj1, obj2)
                        obj1["seen_in_views"] = list(set(obj1.get("seen_in_views", []) + obj2.get("seen_in_views", [])))
                        obj1["_match_scores"] = obj1.get("_match_scores", []) + obj2.get("_match_scores", []) + [0.85]
                        skip_indices.add(j)
            
            final_list.append(obj1)

        # Formatting Output payload attributes
        kept: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        for k_obj in final_list:
            views = sorted(k_obj.get("seen_in_views", []))
            k_obj["merged_from_images"] = views
            
            # Match scores parsing or heuristic confidence based on view counts
            scores = k_obj.pop("_match_scores", [])
            if len(views) >= 2:
                k_obj["merge_confidence"] = round(min(0.95, 0.8 + 0.05 * len(views)), 2)
            else:
                k_obj["merge_confidence"] = 0.70
                
            k_obj.pop("seen_in_views", None)
            k_obj.pop("_normalized_label", None)
            k_obj.pop("_view_source", None)

            # Ensure all required confidence and source attributes are present
            if "estimated_dimensions" not in k_obj or not k_obj["estimated_dimensions"]:
                k_obj["estimated_dimensions"] = {"width_cm": 80, "depth_cm": 80, "height_cm": 80}
            
            if "dimension_confidence" not in k_obj["estimated_dimensions"] or k_obj["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] is None:
                k_obj["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] = 0.35
            if "measurement_source" not in k_obj["estimated_dimensions"] or k_obj["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] is None:
                k_obj["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"

            if "room_position" not in k_obj or not k_obj["room_position"]:
                pos = k_obj.get("position", {"x_cm": 200, "y_cm": 250})
                k_obj["room_position"] = {
                    "x_cm": pos.get("x_cm", 200),
                    "y_cm": pos.get("y_cm", 250),
                    "rotation": k_obj.get("rotation_deg", 0)
                }
            if "depth_confidence" not in k_obj["room_position"] or k_obj["room_position"]["depth_confidence"] is None:
                k_obj["room_position"]["depth_confidence"] = 0.28
            if "position_source" not in k_obj["room_position"] or k_obj["room_position"]["position_source"] is None:
                k_obj["room_position"]["position_source"] = "vision_estimation"

            kept.append(k_obj)

        for o in no_bbox:
            o["merged_from_images"] = [o.get("_view_source", 0)]
            o["merge_confidence"] = 0.70
            o.pop("_normalized_label", None)
            o.pop("_view_source", None)
            
            if "estimated_dimensions" not in o or not o["estimated_dimensions"]:
                o["estimated_dimensions"] = {"width_cm": 80, "depth_cm": 80, "height_cm": 80}
            if "dimension_confidence" not in o["estimated_dimensions"] or o["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] is None:
                o["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] = 0.35
            if "measurement_source" not in o["estimated_dimensions"] or o["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] is None:
                o["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"
                
            kept.append(o)

        return kept

    def merge_pair(self, primary: Dict[str, Any], secondary: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Merge attribute evidence from secondary into primary.

        Prefers primary (highest confidence) values, falling back to secondary if missing.
        """
        merged = dict(primary)
        conf_p = float(primary.get("confidence", 0.5))
        conf_s = float(secondary.get("confidence", 0.5))
        total = conf_p + conf_s or 1.0

        # Confidence: average
        for conf_key in ("confidence", "detection_confidence", "attribute_confidence", "geometry_confidence"):
            val_p = primary.get(conf_key)
            val_s = secondary.get(conf_key)
            if val_p is not None and val_s is not None:
                merged[conf_key] = round((float(val_p) + float(val_s)) / 2, 4)
            elif val_s is not None:
                merged[conf_key] = val_s

        # BBox: weighted average
        if "bbox" in primary and "bbox" in secondary:
            def wav(k: str) -> float:
                return (
                    float(primary["bbox"][k]) * conf_p
                    + float(secondary["bbox"][k]) * conf_s
                ) / total
            merged["bbox"] = {k: round(wav(k), 1) for k in ("x", "y", "width", "height")}

        # Dimensions: weighted average
        if "estimated_dimensions" in primary or "estimated_dimensions" in secondary:
            dims_p = primary.get("estimated_dimensions") or {}
            dims_s = secondary.get("estimated_dimensions") or {}
            merged_dims = {}
            for d in ("width_cm", "depth_cm", "height_cm"):
                vp = dims_p.get(d)
                vs = dims_s.get(d)
                if vp is not None and vs is not None:
                    merged_dims[d] = round((float(vp) * conf_p + float(vs) * conf_s) / total, 1)
                elif vp is not None:
                    merged_dims[d] = vp
                elif vs is not None:
                    merged_dims[d] = vs
            
            # Preserve or compute dimension_confidence
            vp_conf = dims_p.get("dimension_confidence")
            vs_conf = dims_s.get("dimension_confidence")
            if vp_conf is not None and vs_conf is not None:
                merged_dims["dimension_confidence"] = round((float(vp_conf) + float(vs_conf)) / 2, 2)
            elif vp_conf is not None:
                merged_dims["dimension_confidence"] = vp_conf
            elif vs_conf is not None:
                merged_dims["dimension_confidence"] = vs_conf
            else:
                merged_dims["dimension_confidence"] = 0.35
                
            merged_dims["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"
            merged["estimated_dimensions"] = merged_dims

        # Categorical / string attributes
        categorical_attrs = (
            "colour", "color", "material", "condition", "subcategory", 
            "style", "estimated_weight", "visibility", "occluded_by"
        )
        for attr in categorical_attrs:
            val_p = primary.get(attr)
            val_s = secondary.get(attr)
            if val_p in ("unknown", "", None) and val_s not in ("unknown", "", None):
                merged[attr] = val_s

        # Boolean flags
        bool_flags = ("movable", "wall_aligned", "segmentation_available", "mask_available")
        for flag in bool_flags:
            val_p = primary.get(flag)
            val_s = secondary.get(flag)
            if val_p is None:
                merged[flag] = val_s
            elif val_s is not None:
                merged[flag] = val_p or val_s

        return merged

```

## File: `product_classifier.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/utils/product_classifier.py`

```python
import re
from typing import Any, Dict, Tuple

class ProductClassifier:
    @staticmethod
    def normalize_product(product: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """Safely normalizes product dictionary keys and creates a copy with normalized metadata.
        Does not mutate or overwrite original values, but appends normalized fields.
        """
        if not isinstance(product, dict):
            product = {}
        
        prod_copy = dict(product)
        
        prod_name = str(
            prod_copy.get("name")
            or prod_copy.get("title")
            or prod_copy.get("product_name")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        
        prod_cat = str(
            prod_copy.get("category")
            or prod_copy.get("product_category")
            or prod_copy.get("item_type")
            or prod_copy.get("type")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        
        prod_brand = str(
            prod_copy.get("brand")
            or ""
        ).strip()
        
        # Add conformed normalized metadata for internal/downstream use
        prod_copy["normalized_name"] = prod_name.lower()
        prod_copy["normalized_category"] = prod_cat.lower()
        prod_copy["normalized_brand"] = prod_brand.lower()
        
        return prod_copy

    @staticmethod
    def classify(product: Dict[str, Any]) -> Tuple[str, str]:
        """Classifies product into canonical_category and placement_domain."""
        if not product or not isinstance(product, dict):
            return "unknown", "unknown"
            
        prod = ProductClassifier.normalize_product(product)
        name = prod["normalized_name"]
        cat = prod["normalized_category"]
        
        text = f"{name} {cat}".strip()
        
        # 1. Ceiling Fan (Must check phrase/specific keywords first)
        if "ceiling fan" in text or text == "fan" or ("ceiling" in text and "fan" in text):
            return "ceiling_fan", "ceiling"
            
        # 2. Projector Screen (Check before generic projector)
        if "projector screen" in text:
            return "projector_screen", "wall"
            
        # 3. Projector
        if "projector" in text:
            return "projector", "ceiling_or_surface"
            
        # 4. TV Unit
        if any(k in text for k in ["tv unit", "tv stand", "media console", "entertainment unit", "entertainment center"]):
            return "tv_unit", "floor"
            
        # 5. Television (Smart TV, Led TV etc. Check after TV Unit)
        if any(k in text for k in ["television", "smart tv", "led tv", "oled tv", "qled tv"]):
            return "television", "wall_or_surface"
        if re.search(r'\btv\b', text):
            return "television", "wall_or_surface"
            
        # 6. Bookcase / Bookshelf
        if any(k in text for k in ["bookcase", "bookshelf", "book shelf"]):
            return "bookcase", "floor_wall_adjacent"
            
        # 7. Study Table
        if any(k in text for k in ["study table", "computer table", "office desk", "writing desk", "desk"]):
            return "study_table", "floor"
            
        # 8. Office Chair
        if any(k in text for k in ["office chair", "study chair", "ergonomic chair", "computer chair"]):
            return "office_chair", "floor"
            
        # 9. Coffee Maker
        if any(k in text for k in ["coffee maker", "coffee machine"]):
            return "coffee_maker", "support_surface"
            
        # 10. Laptop Holder
        if any(k in text for k in ["laptop holder", "laptop stand"]):
            return "laptop_holder", "support_surface"
            
        # 11. Air Cooler
        if any(k in text for k in ["air cooler", "portable cooler", "cooler"]):
            return "air_cooler", "floor"
            
        # 12. Wall Art
        if any(k in text for k in ["wall art", "artwork", "painting"]):
            return "wall_art", "wall"
            
        # 13. Air Conditioner
        if any(k in text for k in ["air conditioner", "split ac", "wall ac", "conditioner"]):
            return "air_conditioner", "wall"
            
        # Fallback
        orig_cat = product.get("category") or product.get("product_category") or product.get("item_type") or product.get("type") or "unknown"
        return str(orig_cat).strip(), "unknown"

```

# utils/prompt_templates.py
"""
VisionSpace AI — Production Prompt Templates
============================================
Centralised, structured prompt builders for every VisionSpace agent.

Design principles:
  - Every template is a *function*, not a raw string — parameters are
    explicit and type-checked.
  - Templates produce structured prompts that request JSON responses so
    agents can be parsed reliably.
  - Each builder has a ``system`` + ``user`` key to map cleanly to chat APIs.
  - Section markers (### CONTEXT, ### TASK, ### OUTPUT FORMAT) are consistent
    across all templates for easy prompt engineering iteration.

Usage::

    from utils.prompt_templates import vision_analysis_prompt
    messages = vision_analysis_prompt(room_type="bedroom")
    # messages → {"system": "...", "user": "..."}
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from typing import Any

from utils.constants import DESIGN_STYLES, ROOM_TYPES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Shared Boilerplate
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_JSON_REMINDER = (
    "Respond ONLY with a valid JSON object matching the schema above. "
    "Do not include markdown code fences, explanations, or any text outside the JSON."
)

_EXPERT_IDENTITY = (
    "You are VisionSpace AI, an expert interior design assistant powered by "
    "computer vision. You analyse rooms with professional precision and provide "
    "actionable, commercially-aware recommendations."
)


def _section(title: str, body: str) -> str:
    return f"### {title}\n{body.strip()}\n"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 1. Vision Agent — Scene Analysis
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def vision_analysis_prompt(
    room_type: str = "living_room",
    confidence_threshold: float = 0.65,
    max_objects: int = 30,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Vision Agent to generate a comprehensive scene understanding analysis.

    The model should return a highly detailed structured JSON matching the 25-feature
    interior design schema, including room attributes, structural details, object details,
    spatial relationships, floor/wall/ceiling properties, lighting, free space, and safety hazards.

    Args:
        room_type:            Room category hint for context.
        confidence_threshold: Minimum detection confidence to include for objects.
        max_objects:          Upper limit on reported objects.

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    schema = {
        "room_understanding": {
            "room_type": "string (e.g., living_room, bedroom)",
            "room_shape": "string (e.g., rectangular, L-shaped)",
            "room_size": "string (small|medium|large)",
            "room_function": "string",
            "estimated_purpose": "string",

                "blocked": "boolean|null",
                "room_position": {
                    "x_cm": "number|null",
                    "y_cm": "number|null",
                    "rotation": "integer|null",
                    "depth_confidence": "number|null (0.0-1.0)",
                    "position_source": "string (vision_estimation|relative_scale_estimation)"
                },
                "rotation_deg": "integer|null",
                "bbox": {
                    "x": "number",
                    "y": "number",
                    "width": "number",
                    "height": "number"
                }
            }
        ],
        "windows": [
            {
                "id": "string",
                "type": "string",
                "estimated_width_cm": "number|null",
                "estimated_height_cm": "number|null",
                "dimension_confidence": "number|null (0.0-1.0)",
                "measurement_source": "string (vision_estimation)",
                "natural_light_contribution": "string (high|medium|low)",
                "curtains": "boolean|null",
                "blinds": "boolean|null",
                "glass_type": "string|null",
                "room_position": {
                    "x_cm": "number|null",
                    "y_cm": "number|null",
                    "rotation": "integer|null",
                    "depth_confidence": "number|null (0.0-1.0)",
                    "position_source": "string (vision_estimation|relative_scale_estimation)"
                },

                }
            }
        ],
        "image_metadata": [

                "orientation": "string (landscape|portrait)"
            }
        ],
        "scene_coverage_percentage": "integer|null",
        "floor_analysis": {

        },

        },

                    "position_source": "string (vision_estimation|relative_scale_estimation)"
                },
                "rotation_deg": "integer|null",
                "bbox": {

                    "y": "number",
                    "width": "number",

                },

                "mask_available": "boolean|null",
                "visibility": "string (full|partial|occluded)|null",
                "occluded_by": "string|null (object ID)",

            "lighting_temperature": "string (warm|neutral|cool)|null"
        },
        "free_space_analysis": {

            "usable_area_sqm": "number|null",
            "largest_rectangle": {
                "width_cm": "number|null",
