            },
            "free_space_confidence": "number|null (0.0-1.0)"
        },
        "accessibility": {
            "walking_width_cm": "number|null",
            "wheelchair_accessible": "boolean|null",
            "trip_hazards": ["string"]
        },
        "walking_paths": [
            [
                { "x": "number", "y": "number" }
            ]
        ],
        "layout_problems": [
            {
                "severity": "string (low|medium|high)",
                "type": "string",
                "description": "string"
            }
        ],
        "empty_regions": [
            {
                "id": "string",
                "width_cm": "number",
                "depth_cm": "number",
                "suitable_for": ["string"]
            }
        ],
        "candidate_furniture_locations": [
            {
                "location": "string",
                "suitable_for": "string"
            }
        ],
        "style": "string|null",
        "dominant_color_palette": ["string"],
        "overall_aesthetic": "string|null",
        "image_quality": [
            {
                "image_index": "integer",
                "blur": "string (low|medium|high)|null",
                "noise": "string (low|medium|high)|null",
                "exposure": "string (good|underexposed|overexposed)|null",
                "usable": "boolean|null"
            }
        ],
        "safety_analysis": [
            {
                "hazard_type": "string (exposed wires|broken furniture|slippery floor|other)",
                "severity": "string (low|medium|high)",
                "description": "string"
            }
        ]
    }

    system = _expert_system_prompt()
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Room type: **{room_type}**\n"
            f"Analyze the visible environment to perform a complete object inventory and room understanding.\n"
            f"Report up to {max_objects} objects."
        )),
        _section("OBJECT DETECTION RULES", (
            "1. Detect every visible object regardless of importance.\n"
            "2. Do not ignore objects simply because they are small.\n"
            "3. If an object occupies only a small part of the image but remains recognizable, include it.\n"
            "4. Prefer over-detection rather than under-detection.\n"
            "5. If uncertain, include the object with lower confidence.\n"
            "6. Use confidence scores instead of omitting uncertain objects.\n"
            "7. Detect partially visible objects.\n"
            "8. Detect occluded objects if enough evidence exists.\n"
            "9. Detect decorative objects.\n"
            "10. Detect electronics and accessories.\n"
            "11. Detect cables and power equipment whenever visible.\n"
            "12. Detect wall-mounted items.\n"
            "13. Detect ceiling-mounted items.\n"
            "14. Detect floor-level items.\n"
            "15. Detect objects placed on furniture surfaces.\n"
            "16. Detect objects attached to walls.\n"
            "17. Detect objects inside shelves and racks if visible."
        )),
        _section("OBJECT CATEGORIES TO SCAN AND INVENTORY", (
            "### LARGE OBJECTS:\n"
            "Beds, Sofas, Tables, Desks, Wardrobes, Chairs, Bookshelves, TV units, Dining tables, Cabinets, Refrigerators, Washing machines.\n\n"
            "### MEDIUM OBJECTS:\n"
            "Monitors, Laptops, Printers, Floor lamps, Table lamps, Wall clocks, Curtains, Wall decorations, Whiteboards, Plants, Potted plants, Ceiling lights, Mirrors, Side tables.\n\n"
            "### SMALL OBJECTS:\n"
            "Keyboard, Mouse, Mousepad, Books, Notebooks, Pens, Mobile phones, Water bottles, Coffee mugs, Remote controls, Headphones, Chargers, Adapters, Power banks, Extension boards, Power strips, Routers, Speakers, Controllers, Decorative items, Photo frames, Stationery items, Cables, USB drives, Small plants.\n\n"
            "### VERY SMALL OBJECTS:\n"
            "Charging cables, Loose wires, Connectors, Switches, Sockets, Plug points, Adapters, Small decorations, Wall hooks, Door handles."
        )),
        _section("CONFIDENCE POLICY", (
            f"Include objects matching the confidence thresholds:\n"
            f"- Major Furniture: minimum confidence = 0.60\n"
            f"- Medium Objects: minimum confidence = 0.45\n"
            f"- Small Objects: minimum confidence = 0.30\n"
            f"- Very Small Objects: minimum confidence = 0.20\n"
            f"Never discard low confidence small objects. Default to confidence threshold = {confidence_threshold:.2f}."
        )),
        _section("CRITICAL ORIENTATION GUIDELINE", (
            "For every furniture object, door, and window, estimate its precise orientation: rotation_deg "
            "(0, 90, 180, 270) representing facing direction (e.g. bed headboard facing direction, desk seat facing side, "
            "cabinet/wardrobe opening door side, TV screen facing direction). Use these angles: 0 degrees for front facing South, "
            "90 degrees for front facing West, 180 degrees for front facing North, 270 degrees for front facing East."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Spatial Agent — Layout Analysis
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 2. Spatial Agent — Layout Analysis
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def spatial_analysis_prompt(
    room_data: dict[str, Any],
    geometric_report: dict[str, Any],
    proposed_furniture: list[dict] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Spatial Agent to evaluate layout quality, clearances, and placements.

    Args:
        room_data:          Current room layout data (objects, doors, windows, etc.).
        geometric_report:    Factual geometric checks (collisions, clearances, A* paths, balance metrics).
        proposed_furniture:  Optional list of new furniture proposed to fit into the room.

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    room_type = room_data.get("room_understanding", {}).get("room_type", "living_room")
    objects = room_data.get("objects", [])
    doors = room_data.get("doors", [])
    windows = room_data.get("windows", [])
    
    # Text summaries for VLM context
    object_summary = _summarise_objects(objects)
    door_summary = _summarise_list(doors)
    window_summary = _summarise_list(windows)
    prop_summary = _summarise_list(proposed_furniture) if proposed_furniture else "  (none)"
    
    # Geometric checks summary
    collisions = geometric_report.get("collision_report", [])
    clearances = geometric_report.get("clearance_violations", [])
    paths = geometric_report.get("walking_paths", [])
    candidates = geometric_report.get("placement_candidates", [])
    balance = geometric_report.get("balance_score_heuristic", 85)
    symmetry = geometric_report.get("symmetry_score_heuristic", 80)
    
    geom_context = (
        f"- Collision Overlaps Detected: {len(collisions)}\n"
        f"- Clearance Rule Violations: {len(clearances)}\n"
        f"- Calculated Walking Paths: {len(paths)}\n"
        f"- Placement Candidates Found: {len(candidates)}\n"
        f"- Computed Mass Balance Score: {balance:.1f} / 100\n"
        f"- Computed Symmetry Score: {symmetry:.1f} / 100\n"
        f"- Details:\n"
        f"  - Collisions: {[c['description'] for c in collisions[:5]]}\n"
        f"  - Clearances: {[c['description'] for c in clearances[:5]]}"
    )

    schema = {
        "layout_analysis": {
            "layout_score": "integer (0-100)",
            "flow_score": "integer (0-100)",
            "accessibility_score": "integer (0-100)",
            "balance_score": "integer (0-100)",
            "space_utilization_score": "integer (0-100)",
            "focal_point": "string",
            "traffic_flow": "string",
            "functional_zones": ["string"]
        },
        "collision_report": [
            {
                "object1": "string (ID)",
                "object2": "string (ID or door/window)",
                "severity": "string (high|medium)",
                "description": "string"
            }
        ],
        "clearance_violations": [
            {
                "object": "string (ID)",
                "rule": "string",
                "required_clearance_cm": "integer",
                "actual_clearance_cm": "integer",
                "description": "string"
            }
        ],
        "walking_paths": [
            {
                "name": "string (e.g. entrance_to_bed)",
                "walkable": "boolean",
                "length_cm": "integer",
                "bottlenecks": ["string"],
                "coordinates": [{"x": "integer", "y": "integer"}]
            }
        ],
        "placement_candidates": [
            {
                "id": "string",
                "center": {"x_cm": "integer", "y_cm": "integer"},
                "max_dimensions": {"width_cm": "integer", "depth_cm": "integer"},
                "fit_confidence": "number (0.0-1.0)"
            }
        ],
        "furniture_fit_validation": [
            {
                "proposed_id": "string",
                "fits": "boolean",
                "violations": ["string"],
                "preserves_accessibility": "boolean",
                "recommended_position": {"x_cm": "integer", "y_cm": "integer", "rotation": "integer"}
            }
        ],
        "recommendations": [
            {
                "action": "string (move|rotate|relocate|add|remove)",
                "object_id": "string",
                "description": "string",
                "impact": "string (high|medium|low)"
            }
        ],
        "safety_analysis": [
            {
                "hazard": "string",
                "severity": "string (high|medium|low)",
                "mitigation": "string"
            }
        ]
    }

    system = (
        "You are the Spatial Agent for VisionSpace AI, an AI-powered interior planning and space optimization platform. "
        "Your task is to analyze room geometries, clearances, accessibility paths, free space, and safety to perform layout reasoning."
    )
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Room type: **{room_type}**\n"
            f"Objects:\n{object_summary}\n"
            f"Doors:\n{door_summary}\n"
            f"Windows:\n{window_summary}\n"
            f"Proposed additions:\n{prop_summary}"
        )),
        _section("COMPUTED GEOMETRIC REPORT", geom_context),
        _section("TASK", (
            "Analyse the spatial layout of the room using the geometric report as your ground truth. "
            "Evaluate flow, balance, clearances, functional zoning, and calculate scores. "
            "For functional_zones, identify and classify zones dynamically: sleeping, study, entertainment, "
            "dressing, storage, circulation, work, and reading depending on the room objects present. "
            "For proposed additions, validate if they fit. Propose actionable recommendations "
            "to optimize furniture placement and preserve fire exits and emergency paths."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 3. Design Agent — Style & Recommendation
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def design_recommendation_prompt(
    vision_output: dict,
    spatial_output: dict,
    budget: float | int = 50000,
    preferred_style: str = "scandinavian",
    preferred_colors: list[str] | None = None,
    preferred_brands: list[str] | None = None,
    constraints: dict | None = None,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Design Agent to recommend design improvements based on the Master Prompt.

    Args:
        vision_output:    Scene graph/metadata output from the Vision Agent.
        spatial_output:   Geometric layout checks and reports from the Spatial Agent.
        budget:           Total currency units budget for layout enhancements.
        preferred_style:  Target interior design style.
        preferred_colors: List of color preferences.
        preferred_brands: List of preferred manufacturers/brands.
        constraints:      Custom layout/lifestyle constraints.

    Returns:
        Dict with "system" and "user" prompt strings.
    """
    pref_colors = preferred_colors or []
    pref_brands = preferred_brands or []
    user_constraints = constraints or {}

    # Extract merged scene if nested
    v_scene = vision_output.get("merged_scene") if isinstance(vision_output, dict) and "merged_scene" in vision_output else vision_output
    if not isinstance(v_scene, dict):
        v_scene = {}

    # Minify vision_output to keep KV cache minimal and prevent OOM
    min_vision = {
        "room_type": v_scene.get("room_understanding", {}).get("room_type"),
        "room_size": v_scene.get("room_understanding", {}).get("room_size"),
        "architectural_style": v_scene.get("room_understanding", {}).get("architectural_style"),
        "objects": [
            {
                "id": obj.get("id"),
                "name": obj.get("name"),
                "category": obj.get("category"),
                "subcategory": obj.get("subcategory"),
                "color": obj.get("color"),
                "estimated_dimensions": obj.get("estimated_dimensions")
            }
            for obj in v_scene.get("objects", [])
        ]
    }

    # Minify spatial_output
    min_spatial = {
        "layout_score": spatial_output.get("layout_analysis", {}).get("layout_score") if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else None,
        "flow_score": spatial_output.get("layout_analysis", {}).get("flow_score") if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else None,
        "accessibility_score": spatial_output.get("layout_analysis", {}).get("accessibility_score") if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else None,
        "collision_report": [
            {
                "object1": c.get("object1"),
                "object2": c.get("object2"),
                "severity": c.get("severity"),
                "description": c.get("description")
            }
            for c in spatial_output.get("collision_report", [])
        ] if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else [],
        "clearance_violations": [
            {
                "object": v.get("object"),
                "rule": v.get("rule"),
                "description": v.get("description")
            }
            for v in spatial_output.get("clearance_violations", [])
        ] if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else [],
        "walking_paths": [
            {
                "name": p.get("name"),
                "walkable": p.get("walkable"),
                "length_cm": p.get("length_cm"),
                "bottlenecks": p.get("bottlenecks")
            }
            for p in spatial_output.get("walking_paths", [])
        ] if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else [],
        "placement_candidates": [
            {
                "id": c.get("id"),
                "center": c.get("center"),
                "max_dimensions": c.get("max_dimensions")
            }
            for c in spatial_output.get("placement_candidates", [])
        ] if isinstance(spatial_output, dict) else []
    }

    # Try to load and render prompts/design_prompt.txt if it exists
    import pathlib
    import json
    p_path = pathlib.Path("prompts/design_prompt.txt")
    if p_path.exists():
        try:
            raw_template = p_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            from providers.huggingface_provider import format_prompt
            user = format_prompt(raw_template, {
                "vision_output": json.dumps(min_vision, indent=2),
                "spatial_output": json.dumps(min_spatial, indent=2),
                "budget": budget,
                "preferred_style": preferred_style,
                "preferred_colors": pref_colors,
                "preferred_brands": pref_brands,
                "constraints": json.dumps(user_constraints, indent=2)
            })
            system = "You are the Design Agent for VisionSpace AI, an AI-powered interior planning and space optimization platform. Act as a professional interior designer."
            return {"system": system, "user": user}
        except Exception:
            pass

    schema = {
        "design_theme": "string",
        "design_style": "string",
        "room_goal": "string",
        "materials": {
            "primary_material": "string",
            "secondary_material": "string",
            "accent_material": "string"
        },
        "lighting_strategy": {
            "lighting_mood": "string",
            "color_temperature": "string",
            "natural_light_strategy": "string"
        },
        "color_palette": {
            "primary_colors": ["string"],
            "secondary_colors": ["string"],
            "accent_colors": ["string"],
            "wall_colors": ["string"],
            "furniture_colors": ["string"]
        },
        "recommended_furniture": [
            {
                "id": "string",
                "name": "string",
                "category": "string",
                "priority": "string (Mandatory|Recommended|Optional|Luxury)",
                "estimated_cost": "number",
                "retain_existing": "boolean",
                "reason": "string"
            }
        ],
        "layout_plan": [
            {
                "furniture_id": "string",
                "position": {
                    "x_cm": "integer",
                    "y_cm": "integer"
                },
                "rotation": "integer (0|90|180|270)",
                "zone": "string",
                "clearance_valid": "boolean"
            }
        ],
        "functional_zones": ["string"],
        "budget_breakdown": {
            "furniture": "number",
            "lighting": "number",
            "decor": "number",
            "storage": "number",
            "buffer": "number",
            "total": "number"
        },
        "shopping_requirements": [
            {
                "category": "string",
                "required_width_cm": "number",
                "required_depth_cm": "number",
                "style": "string",
                "material": "string",
                "budget_limit": "number",
                "preferred_brands": ["string"]
            }
        ],
        "estimated_total_cost": "number",
        "design_explanations": ["string"],
        "confidence_score": "number (0.0-1.0)"
    }

    system = (
        "You are the Design Agent for VisionSpace AI, an AI-powered interior planning and space optimization platform. "
        "Your task is to act as a professional interior designer. "
        "Your responsibility is NOT object detection and NOT geometry computation; use the provided inputs as your factual layout ground truth. "
        "Generate a comprehensive, optimized interior design plan that maximizes usability, aesthetics, comfort, "
        "accessibility, circulation efficiency, natural lighting, storage efficiency, budget efficiency, and visual balance."
    )

    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("INPUT", json.dumps({
            "vision_output": min_vision,
            "spatial_output": min_spatial,
            "budget": budget,
            "preferred_style": preferred_style,
            "preferred_colors": pref_colors,
            "preferred_brands": pref_brands,
            "constraints": user_constraints
        }, indent=2)),
        _section("STYLE UNDERSTANDING", (
            "Scandinavian: oak wood, white walls, neutral colors, minimal clutter, natural light\n"
            "Modern: clean geometry, simple furniture, neutral palette, hidden storage\n"
            "Minimalist: fewer furniture items, open circulation, functional furniture only\n"
            "Industrial: black metal, exposed materials, darker tones, raw textures\n"
            "Japandi: warm wood, soft colors, low-profile furniture, calm atmosphere\n"
            "Luxury: premium materials, layered lighting, statement pieces, rich textures\n"
            "Bohemian: colorful accents, organic materials, decorative layering\n"
            "Traditional: classic furniture, warm colors, detailed finishes\n"
            "Contemporary: mixed materials, adaptive layouts, modern lighting"
        )),
        _section("MINIMUM CLEARANCES", (
            "Bed clearance: 60 cm\n"
            "Desk clearance: 75 cm\n"
            "Wardrobe opening clearance: 90 cm\n"
            "Door swing clearance: 90 cm\n"
            "Main walking path: 80 cm minimum\n"
            "Wheelchair access: 90 cm minimum"
        )),
        _section("BUDGET RULES", (
            "Distribute budget across: furniture, lighting, storage, decor, accessories, and a 5% to 10% contingency buffer.\n"
            "Never exceed the total budget."
        )),
        _section("TASKS", (
            "1. Generate the room theme and style based on preferred style and color presets.\n"
            "2. Define primary, secondary, and accent materials, textures, and lighting mood.\n"
            "3. Select furniture and prioritize them (Mandatory, Recommended, Optional, Luxury).\n"
            "4. Determine furniture positions (x_cm, y_cm, rotation) and corresponding zones (sleeping, workspace, storage, circulation, etc.).\n"
            "5. Build shopping requirements for each piece (category, dimensions, brand constraints).\n"
            "6. Explain all recommendations across style match, usability, circulation, aesthetics, and budget categories."
        )),
        _section("STRICT RULES", (
            "- Never recommend furniture that does not fit.\n"
            "- Never violate spatial constraints.\n"
            "- Never exceed budget.\n"
            "- Never block windows or doors.\n"
            "- Never reduce accessibility.\n"
            "- Prefer multifunctional furniture whenever possible.\n"
            "- Prefer natural lighting.\n"
            "- Minimize clutter."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 4. Commerce Agent — Product Search & Ranking
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def commerce_search_prompt(
    design_changes: list[dict],
    budget_tier: str = "mid_range",
    style: str = "modern",
    max_results_per_item: int = 3,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Commerce Agent to generate product search queries.

    Args:
        design_changes:      List of design change dicts from Design Agent.
        budget_tier:         Budget tier for price filtering.
        style:               Interior style to guide product selection.
        max_results_per_item: Max products to suggest per change item.

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    changes_text = "\n".join(
        f"  {i+1}. [{c.get('type','?').upper()}] {c.get('description','?')}"
        for i, c in enumerate(design_changes[:15])
    )
    schema = {
        "product_searches": [
            {
                "change_index":   "int (1-indexed)",
                "search_queries": ["string (2-5 word product search queries)"],
                "category":       "string",
                "price_range":    {"min": "int USD", "max": "int USD"},
                "style_keywords": ["string"],
                "avoid_keywords": ["string"],
            }
        ]
    }

    system = _expert_system_prompt()
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Style: **{style}** | Budget: **{budget_tier}**\n"
            f"Design changes to source products for:\n{changes_text}"
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            "For each design change, generate targeted product search queries "
            f"that will find suitable replacements. Suggest up to "
            f"{max_results_per_item} search queries per item. Include "
            "style-appropriate keywords and exclude terms that don't fit the aesthetic."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 5. Budget Agent — Cost Estimation & Breakdown
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def budget_estimation_prompt(
    design_changes: list[dict],
    products: list[dict],
    total_budget: float,
    currency: str = "USD",
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Budget Agent to allocate costs and prioritise spending.

    Args:
        design_changes: Design change list.
        products:       Product candidates from Commerce Agent.
        total_budget:   User's stated total budget.
        currency:       Currency code (default USD).

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    changes_text = _summarise_list(design_changes, max_items=20)
    schema = {
        "budget_plan": {
            "total_budget":    f"float {currency}",
            "allocated_total": f"float {currency}",
            "contingency":     f"float {currency} (10% recommended)",
            "items": [
                {
                    "change_index":  "int",
                    "description":   "string",
                    "priority":      "int 1-5",
                    "estimated_cost": f"float {currency}",
                    "product_name":  "string (recommended product)",
                    "retailer":      "string",
                    "url_hint":      "string (search term)",
                    "phase":         "int (1=immediate, 2=soon, 3=optional)",
                }
            ],
            "phases": {
                "phase_1_total": f"float {currency}",
                "phase_2_total": f"float {currency}",
                "phase_3_total": f"float {currency}",
            },
            "savings_tips": ["string"],
            "splurge_items": ["string (items worth spending more on)"],
        }
    }

    system = _expert_system_prompt()
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Total budget: **{currency} {total_budget:,.0f}**\n"
            f"Number of changes: {len(design_changes)}\n"
            f"Changes:\n{changes_text}"
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            f"Allocate the {currency} {total_budget:,.0f} budget across all design "
            "changes. Group items into phases (1=immediate, 2=soon, 3=optional). "
            "Reserve 10% as contingency. Identify items where quality matters most "
            "(splurge) and where budget alternatives work fine (save). "
            "Provide 3-5 money-saving tips."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 6. Explain Agent — Natural Language Summary
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def explain_design_prompt(
    vision_output: dict,
    spatial_output: dict,
    design_plan: dict,
    audience: str = "homeowner",
    tone: str = "warm_professional",
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt for the Explain Agent to produce a human-readable design summary.

    Args:
        vision_output:  Raw Vision Agent output.
        spatial_output: Spatial Agent layout analysis.
        design_plan:    Design Agent recommendations.
        audience:       Target reader (homeowner|interior_designer|real_estate_agent).
        tone:           Writing tone (warm_professional|technical|casual).

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    vision_summary = _summarise_dict(vision_output, max_keys=5)
    spatial_summary = _summarise_dict(spatial_output, max_keys=5)
    design_summary = _summarise_dict(design_plan, max_keys=6)

    schema = {
        "explanation": {
            "headline":         "string (compelling one-liner)",
            "current_state":    "string (2-3 sentences about existing room)",
            "key_issues":       ["string (brief issue descriptions)"],
            "transformation":   "string (3-4 sentences on the proposed transformation)",
            "top_3_changes":    ["string (action-oriented, specific)"],
            "style_description": "string (evocative style narrative)",
            "before_after":     "string (contrast sentence)",
            "call_to_action":   "string (encouraging closing statement)",
        }
    }

    system = (
        f"You are VisionSpace AI, an expert interior design communicator. "
        f"Your audience is a **{audience}**. Use a **{tone}** tone. "
        f"Write clearly, engagingly, and avoid jargon unless the audience is a professional."
    )
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Vision analysis highlights:\n{vision_summary}\n\n"
            f"Spatial analysis highlights:\n{spatial_summary}\n\n"
            f"Design plan highlights:\n{design_summary}"
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            "Write a compelling, human-readable summary of the room analysis and "
            "design recommendations. Use vivid but concise language. "
            "Make the homeowner excited about the transformation."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 7. Object Enrichment Prompt
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def object_enrichment_prompt(
    label: str,
    colour: str,
    material: str,
    room_type: str,
    style: str,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt to enrich a single detected object with design metadata.

    Args:
        label:     Object label (e.g. "sofa").
        colour:    Detected colour string.
        material:  Detected material string.
        room_type: Room context.
        style:     Current or target design style.

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    schema = {
        "enriched_object": {
            "label":              label,
            "style_compatibility": ["string (compatible styles)"],
            "typical_dimensions": {"width_cm": "int", "depth_cm": "int", "height_cm": "int"},
            "material_quality":   "string (budget|mid|premium|luxury)",
            "age_estimate":       "string (new|1-5 years|5-10 years|vintage|antique)",
            "replacement_priority": "int 1-5 (1=replace soon, 5=keep)",
            "keep_or_replace":    "string (keep|replace|refinish|reupholster)",
            "reason":             "string",
            "upgrade_options":    ["string (upgrade path descriptions)"],
            "accessory_pairings": ["string (complementary items)"],
        }
    }

    system = _expert_system_prompt()
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Object: **{label}** | Colour: {colour} | Material: {material}\n"
            f"Room: {room_type} | Style target: {style}"
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            f"Provide expert design metadata for this {label}. Assess its style "
            "compatibility, estimate its dimensions and quality tier, and recommend "
            "whether to keep, replace, or refinish it. Suggest accessories that pair well."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# 8. Visualisation Prompt (Inpainting Direction)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def visualisation_prompt(
    design_changes: list[dict],
    style: str,
    colour_palette: dict,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Prompt to generate an inpainting / image-generation direction string.

    This is fed to the Visualisation Agent which constructs prompts for
    Stable Diffusion or similar generative models.

    Returns:
        Dict with ``"system"`` and ``"user"`` prompt strings.
    """
    changes_text = "; ".join(
        c.get("description", "") for c in design_changes[:8]
    )
    palette_text = ", ".join(f"{k}: {v}" for k, v in colour_palette.items())
    schema = {
        "visualisation": {
            "positive_prompt":  "string (SD-style prompt, comma-separated)",
            "negative_prompt":  "string (SD-style negative prompt)",
            "style_tokens":     ["string"],
            "colour_tokens":    ["string"],
            "inpaint_regions": [
                {
                    "object_id":     "string",
                    "replacement":   "string",
                    "prompt_suffix": "string",
                }
            ],
            "guidance_scale":   "float (7.5-15.0 recommended)",
            "steps":            "int (20-50 recommended)",
        }
    }

    system = (
        "You are an expert in prompt engineering for interior design image generation. "
        "You create precise, style-accurate prompts for Stable Diffusion models."
    )
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Style: **{style}** | Colour palette: {palette_text}\n"
            f"Design changes: {changes_text}"
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            "Generate a detailed Stable Diffusion prompt for visualising the "
            "proposed room transformation. Include positive and negative prompts, "
            "style tokens, colour tokens, and per-object inpainting instructions."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])

    return {"system": system, "user": user}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Private Helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def _expert_system_prompt() -> str:
    return (
        f"{_EXPERT_IDENTITY}\n\n"
        "CRITICAL SYSTEM RULES:\n"
        "0. CONCISE THINKING: Keep your internal reasoning/thinking process inside the <think>...</think> tag extremely brief, concise, and direct (under 5 sentences). Do not write a long essay in thinking.\n"
        "1. DOOR AND WINDOW DETECTION: You must identify every visible door and window in the scene. "
        "Do not ignore doors, glass doors, sliding doors, windows, skylights, or archways. You MUST populate "
        "both the 'doors' and 'windows' lists in the JSON structure with their estimated dimensions and 2D bounding boxes.\n"
        "2. SCENE BOUNDARIES & OBJECTS: Detect all core furniture items. Ensure that for each item "
        "you provide high-confidence labels, styles, materials, colors, and estimated dimensions.\n"
        "3. SPATIAL POSITIONING & ROTATION: You must estimate relative physical room coordinates "
        "under 'room_position' (x_cm, y_cm relative to the room floor corner) and 'rotation_deg' (estimated rotation in degrees, e.g. 0, 90, 180, 270) based on room cues.\n"
        "4. MULTI-VIEW CONSISTENCY: If analyzing multiple viewpoint images of the same room, understand "
        "that the same physical object will appear from different camera angles. Ensure that when you identify objects, "
        "you represent the physical room's layout. Keep description details, colors, styles, and IDs consistent "
        "across images so the post-processing engine can merge them cleanly into one canonical object."
    )


def _summarise_objects(objects: list[dict], max_items: int = 20) -> str:
    lines = []
    for i, obj in enumerate(objects[:max_items]):
        label = obj.get("label", "?")
        colour = obj.get("colour", "")
        bbox = obj.get("bbox", {})
        lines.append(f"  [{i+1}] {label} ({colour}) bbox={bbox}")
    if len(objects) > max_items:
        lines.append(f"  … and {len(objects) - max_items} more objects")
    return "\n".join(lines) if lines else "  (none)"


def _summarise_dict(d: dict, max_keys: int = 6) -> str:
    if not d:
        return "  (empty)"
    lines = []
    for i, (k, v) in enumerate(d.items()):
        if i >= max_keys:
            lines.append(f"  … ({len(d) - max_keys} more keys)")
            break
        if isinstance(v, (list, dict)):
            v_str = json.dumps(v)[:80] + ("…" if len(json.dumps(v)) > 80 else "")
        else:
            v_str = str(v)
        lines.append(f"  {k}: {v_str}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def _summarise_list(items: list, max_items: int = 15) -> str:
    lines = []
    for i, item in enumerate(items[:max_items]):
        if isinstance(item, dict):
            desc = item.get("description", str(item)[:60])
        else:
            desc = str(item)[:60]
        lines.append(f"  [{i+1}] {desc}")
    if len(items) > max_items:
        lines.append(f"  … and {len(items) - max_items} more")
    return "\n".join(lines) if lines else "  (none)"


def vision_tile_analysis_prompt(
    room_type: str = "living_room",
    tile_index: int = 1,
    confidence_threshold: float = 0.35,
    max_objects: int = 25,
) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Targeted prompt to detect fine-grained local items inside a specific tile quadrant."""
    schema = {
        "objects": [
            {
                "name": "string (e.g. Office Chair)",
                "bbox": [100, 150, 40, 60],
                "confidence": 0.85
            }
        ]
    }
    system = _expert_system_prompt()
    user = "\n\n".join([
        _section("CONTEXT", (
            f"Room type: {room_type}\n"
            f"You are analyzing a cropped tile quadrant (quadrant index: {tile_index}) of a larger high-resolution room image. "
            f"Focus on identifying small objects, workspace components, decor, and fittings in this local area. "
            f"Include only objects with confidence >= {confidence_threshold:.2f}."
        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            "Analyze the cropped image segment and list all visible furniture, electronics, fixtures, decor, and accessories. "
            "CRITICAL: MAXIMUM RECALL OBJECT DETECTION MANDATE\n"
            "Document every single item no matter how small, secondary, or mundane. "
            "Scan for: laptops, monitors, mouse, keyboards, desk accessories, lamps, cables, chargers, plant pots, potted plants, books, organizers, cups, wall art. "
            "Provide coordinates [x, y, width, height] relative to the cropped tile dimensions."
        )),
        _section("OUTPUT FORMAT", json.dumps(schema, indent=2)),
        _JSON_REMINDER,
    ])
    return {"system": system, "user": user}


def vision_crop_analysis_prompt(
    furniture_name: str,
    room_type: str = "living_room",
    confidence_threshold: float = 0.35,
    max_objects: int = 25,

    schema = {
        "objects": [

                "name": "string (e.g. MacBook Pro)",
                "bbox": [100, 150, 40, 60],

        )),
        _section("TASK", (
            f"Scan on, under, inside, or immediately next to the {furniture_name} to locate and document every item. "
            "CRITICAL: MAXIMUM RECALL OBJECT DETECTION MANDATE\n"
            "Identify workspace/electronic accessories, decor, and small items down to the smallest detail. "
            "Specifically look for and list: laptops, desktops, keyboards, mouse, charging cables, power adaptors, books, cups, desk lamps, plant pots, potted plants, decorations. "
            "Provide coordinates [x, y, width, height] relative to this close-up cropped image."
        )),

        _JSON_REMINDER,

    return {"system": system, "user": user}

## File: `room_mapper.py`

```python
# utils/room_mapper.py
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class RoomMapper:
    """Estimates room boundaries, projects object boxes to physical space,

    and generates spatial relationship graphs.
    """

    def map_room_coordinates(
        self, base_scene: Dict[str, Any], doors: List[Dict[str, Any]], windows: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Tuple[int, int, int]:
        """Projects image-level bounding boxes into relative physical coordinates in centimeters.

        Origin (0, 0, 0) is the front-left floor corner.
        Returns:
            room_width, room_depth, room_height
        """
        # 1. Determine Room Bounds
        r_size = base_scene.get("room_understanding", {}).get("room_size", "medium").lower()
        if r_size == "large":
            room_width, room_depth, room_height = 500, 600, 260
        elif r_size == "small":
            room_width, room_depth, room_height = 300, 400, 240
        else:
            room_width, room_depth, room_height = 400, 500, 240

        # 2. Get image dimensions from metadata
        img_w, img_h = 800, 600
        meta = base_scene.get("image_metadata", [])
        if meta and isinstance(meta, list) and isinstance(meta[0], dict):
            img_w = meta[0].get("image_width") or 800
            img_h = meta[0].get("image_height") or 600

        # 3. Project objects
        for obj in base_scene.get("objects", []):
            bbox = obj.get("bbox", {"x": 100, "y": 100, "width": 200, "height": 200})
            cx = bbox["x"] + bbox["width"] / 2
            cy = bbox["y"] + bbox["height"] / 2

            model_pos = obj.get("room_position")
            if isinstance(model_pos, dict) and model_pos.get("x_cm") is not None and model_pos.get("y_cm") is not None:
                x_cm = int(model_pos["x_cm"])
                y_cm = int(model_pos["y_cm"])
                rotation_deg = int(model_pos.get("rotation") or obj.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "vision_estimation"
            else:
                x_cm = int((cx / img_w) * room_width)
                y_cm = int((cy / img_h) * room_depth)

                orient = str(obj.get("orientation", "")).lower()
                if "vertical" in orient or "north" in orient or "south" in orient:
                    rotation_deg = 90
                elif "east" in orient or "west" in orient:
                    rotation_deg = 0
                else:
                    rotation_deg = 90 if "desk" in obj.get("name", "").lower() or "dresser" in obj.get("name", "").lower() else 0
                pos_source = "relative_scale_estimation"

            # Clamping coordinates within physical walls
            x_cm = max(10, min(room_width - 10, x_cm))
            y_cm = max(10, min(room_depth - 10, y_cm))

            # Override with direct VLM rotation_deg if present
            model_rot = obj.get("rotation_deg")
            if model_rot is not None:
                try:
                    rotation_deg = int(model_rot)
                except ValueError:
                    pass

            name_cat = f"{obj.get('name', '')} {obj.get('category', '')}".lower()
            if "lamp" in name_cat or "sconce" in name_cat or "light" in name_cat:
                if "table" in name_cat or "desk" in name_cat:
                    z_cm = 75
                elif "ceiling" in name_cat:
                    z_cm = 220
                else:
                    z_cm = 120
            elif "painting" in name_cat or "frame" in name_cat or "decor" in name_cat:
                z_cm = 140
            else:
                z_cm = 0

            # Calculate confidences
            det_conf = float(obj.get("confidence", 0.7))
            dim_conf = round(det_conf * 0.9, 2)
            depth_conf = round(1.0 - (y_cm / room_depth) * 0.5, 2)

            obj["position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "z_cm": z_cm
            }
            obj["room_position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "rotation": rotation_deg,
                "depth_confidence": depth_conf,
                "position_source": pos_source
            }
            obj["room_coordinates"] = [round(x_cm / 100.0, 2), round(y_cm / 100.0, 2), round(z_cm / 100.0, 2)]
            obj["rotation_deg"] = rotation_deg

            if "estimated_dimensions" not in obj or not obj["estimated_dimensions"]:
                obj["estimated_dimensions"] = {"width_cm": 80, "depth_cm": 80, "height_cm": 80}
            obj["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] = dim_conf
            obj["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"

        # 4. Project doors
        for door in doors:
            bbox = door.get("bbox", {"x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100})
            cx = bbox["x"] + bbox["width"] / 2
            cy = bbox["y"] + bbox["height"] / 2
            
            model_pos = door.get("room_position")
            if isinstance(model_pos, dict) and model_pos.get("x_cm") is not None and model_pos.get("y_cm") is not None:
                x_cm = int(model_pos["x_cm"])
                y_cm = int(model_pos["y_cm"])
                rotation_deg = int(model_pos.get("rotation") or door.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "vision_estimation"
            else:
                x_cm = int((cx / img_w) * room_width)
                y_cm = int((cy / img_h) * room_depth)
                rotation_deg = int(door.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "relative_scale_estimation"

            # Clamping
            x_cm = max(10, min(room_width - 10, x_cm))
            y_cm = max(10, min(room_depth - 10, y_cm))
            
            det_conf = float(door.get("confidence", 0.7))
            dim_conf = round(det_conf * 0.9, 2)
            depth_conf = round(1.0 - (y_cm / room_depth) * 0.5, 2)
            
            door["position"] = {"x_cm": x_cm, "y_cm": y_cm, "z_cm": 0}
            door["room_position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "rotation": rotation_deg,
                "depth_confidence": depth_conf,
                "position_source": pos_source
            }
            door["room_coordinates"] = [round(x_cm / 100.0, 2), round(y_cm / 100.0, 2), 0.0]
            door["rotation_deg"] = rotation_deg
            door["dimension_confidence"] = dim_conf
            door["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"

        # 5. Project windows
        for window in windows:
            bbox = window.get("bbox", {"x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100})
            cx = bbox["x"] + bbox["width"] / 2
            cy = bbox["y"] + bbox["height"] / 2
            
            model_pos = window.get("room_position")
            if isinstance(model_pos, dict) and model_pos.get("x_cm") is not None and model_pos.get("y_cm") is not None:
                x_cm = int(model_pos["x_cm"])
                y_cm = int(model_pos["y_cm"])
                rotation_deg = int(model_pos.get("rotation") or window.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "vision_estimation"
            else:
                x_cm = int((cx / img_w) * room_width)
                y_cm = int((cy / img_h) * room_depth)
                rotation_deg = int(window.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "relative_scale_estimation"

            # Clamping
            x_cm = max(10, min(room_width - 10, x_cm))
            y_cm = max(10, min(room_depth - 10, y_cm))
            
            det_conf = float(window.get("confidence", 0.7))
            dim_conf = round(det_conf * 0.9, 2)
            depth_conf = round(1.0 - (y_cm / room_depth) * 0.5, 2)
            
            window["position"] = {"x_cm": x_cm, "y_cm": y_cm, "z_cm": 100}
            window["room_position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "rotation": rotation_deg,
                "depth_confidence": depth_conf,
                "position_source": pos_source
            }
            window["room_coordinates"] = [round(x_cm / 100.0, 2), round(y_cm / 100.0, 2), 1.0]
            window["rotation_deg"] = rotation_deg
            window["dimension_confidence"] = dim_conf
            window["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"

        return room_width, room_depth, room_height

    def map_single_scene_coordinates(
        self, scene: Dict[str, Any], room_width: int, room_depth: int, img_w: int = 800, img_h: int = 600
    ) -> None:
        """Projects room coordinates for a single scene prior to fusion."""
        for obj in scene.get("objects", []):
            bbox = obj.get("bbox", {"x": 100, "y": 100, "width": 200, "height": 200})
            cx = bbox["x"] + bbox["width"] / 2
            cy = bbox["y"] + bbox["height"] / 2
            
            model_pos = obj.get("room_position")
            if isinstance(model_pos, dict) and model_pos.get("x_cm") is not None and model_pos.get("y_cm") is not None:
                x_cm = int(model_pos["x_cm"])
                y_cm = int(model_pos["y_cm"])
                rotation_deg = int(model_pos.get("rotation") or obj.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "vision_estimation"
            else:
                x_cm = int((cx / img_w) * room_width)
                y_cm = int((cy / img_h) * room_depth)
                rotation_deg = int(obj.get("rotation_deg") or 0)
                pos_source = "relative_scale_estimation"

            # Clamping
            x_cm = max(10, min(room_width - 10, x_cm))
            y_cm = max(10, min(room_depth - 10, y_cm))
                
            name_cat = f"{obj.get('name', '')} {obj.get('category', '')}".lower()
            if "lamp" in name_cat or "sconce" in name_cat or "light" in name_cat:
                z_cm = 75 if ("table" in name_cat or "desk" in name_cat) else 120
            else:
                z_cm = 0
                
            det_conf = float(obj.get("confidence", 0.7))
            dim_conf = round(det_conf * 0.9, 2)
            depth_conf = round(1.0 - (y_cm / room_depth) * 0.5, 2)

            obj["position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "z_cm": z_cm
            }
            obj["room_position"] = {
                "x_cm": x_cm,
                "y_cm": y_cm,
                "rotation": rotation_deg,
                "depth_confidence": depth_conf,
                "position_source": pos_source
            }
            obj["room_coordinates"] = [round(x_cm / 100.0, 2), round(y_cm / 100.0, 2), round(z_cm / 100.0, 2)]
            obj["rotation_deg"] = rotation_deg
            
            if "estimated_dimensions" not in obj or not obj["estimated_dimensions"]:
                obj["estimated_dimensions"] = {"width_cm": 80, "depth_cm": 80, "height_cm": 80}
            obj["estimated_dimensions"]["dimension_confidence"] = dim_conf
            obj["estimated_dimensions"]["measurement_source"] = "vision_estimation"

    def generate_spatial_relationships(
        self, objects: List[Dict[str, Any]], doors: List[Dict[str, Any]], windows: List[Dict[str, Any]], room_width: int, room_depth: int, img_w: int = 800
    ) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Calculates distance matrices and adds geometric relationships."""
        relationships: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        for i in range(len(objects)):
            for j in range(i + 1, len(objects)):
                o1 = objects[i]
                o2 = objects[j]
                
                pos1 = o1.get("position", {"x_cm": 0, "y_cm": 0, "z_cm": 0})
                pos2 = o2.get("position", {"x_cm": 0, "y_cm": 0, "z_cm": 0})
                
                dx = pos1["x_cm"] - pos2["x_cm"]
                dy = pos1["y_cm"] - pos2["y_cm"]
                
                if dx > 80:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o1["id"], "object2": o2["id"], "relation": "right_of"})
                    relationships.append({"object1": o2["id"], "object2": o1["id"], "relation": "left_of"})
                elif dx < -80:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o1["id"], "object2": o2["id"], "relation": "left_of"})
                    relationships.append({"object1": o2["id"], "object2": o1["id"], "relation": "right_of"})
                    
                if dy > 80:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o1["id"], "object2": o2["id"], "relation": "behind"})
                    relationships.append({"object1": o2["id"], "object2": o1["id"], "relation": "in_front_of"})
                elif dy < -80:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o1["id"], "object2": o2["id"], "relation": "in_front_of"})
                    relationships.append({"object1": o2["id"], "object2": o1["id"], "relation": "behind"})

        # Alignments with walls
        for o in objects:
            pos = o.get("position", {"x_cm": 0, "y_cm": 0})
            if pos["x_cm"] < 80:
                relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": "wall_west", "relation": "against_wall"})
            if pos["x_cm"] > (room_width - 80):
                relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": "wall_east", "relation": "against_wall"})
            if pos["y_cm"] > (room_depth - 80):
                relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": "wall_north", "relation": "against_wall"})
            if pos["y_cm"] < 80:
                relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": "wall_south", "relation": "against_wall"})
                
            # Windows and Doors proximity
            for w in windows:
                w_pos_x = int((w.get("bbox", {}).get("x", 0) / img_w) * room_width)
                if abs(pos["x_cm"] - w_pos_x) < 150:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": w["id"], "relation": "near_window"})
            for d in doors:
                d_pos_x = int((d.get("bbox", {}).get("x", 0) / img_w) * room_width)
                if abs(pos["x_cm"] - d_pos_x) < 150:
                    relationships.append({"object1": o["id"], "object2": d["id"], "relation": "near_door"})

        return relationships

```

## File: `session_manager.py`
Path: `file:///d:/VisionAI-PRO-AgenticAI/VisionSpace-AI/utils/session_manager.py`

```python
"""Session Manager Module for VisionSpace AI.

Manages chat session history in a thread-safe manner, keeping track of conversation contexts.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import threading
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Optional

logger = logging.getLogger("VisionSpaceAI.SessionManager")


class SessionManager:
    """Manages chat session histories in memory for conversational context."""

    def __init__(self) -> None:
        """Initializes the SessionManager with a lock and session history storage."""
        self._sessions: Dict[str, List[Dict[str, Any]]] = {}
        self._lock = threading.Lock()
        logger.info("SessionManager initialized.")

    def get_session(self, session_id: str) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]:
        """Retrieves history for a given session ID in a thread-safe manner.

        Args:
            session_id: Unique string identifier for the chat session.

        Returns:
            List of message dictionaries representing session conversation history.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if session_id not in self._sessions:
                self._sessions[session_id] = []
            # Return a copy of the list to prevent external mutation side-effects
            return list(self._sessions[session_id])

    def add_message(self, session_id: str, role: str, content: Any, images: Optional[List[Any]] = None) -> None:
        """Appends a new message to the session history in a thread-safe manner.

        Args:
            session_id: Unique string identifier for the chat session.
            role: The author role of the message (e.g. 'user', 'assistant', 'system').
            content: The text content or structured list of the message.
            images: Optional list of images or URLs associated with the message.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if session_id not in self._sessions:
                self._sessions[session_id] = []
            msg = {"role": role, "content": content}
            if images is not None:
                msg["images"] = images
            self._sessions[session_id].append(msg)
            logger.debug("Added message for session_id '%s' (role: %s, with %d images)", session_id, role, len(images) if images else 0)

    def set_session_history(self, session_id: str, history: List[Dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
        """Overwrites or sets the history for a given session ID.

        Args:
            session_id: Unique string identifier for the chat session.
            history: List of message dictionaries representing conversation history.
        """
        with self._lock:
            self._sessions[session_id] = list(history)
            logger.debug("Set session history for session_id '%s' with %d messages", session_id, len(history))

    def clear_session(self, session_id: str) -> None:
        """Clears the history for a session ID.

        Args:
            session_id: Unique string identifier for the chat session.
        """
        with self._lock:
            if session_id in self._sessions:
                del self._sessions[session_id]
                logger.info("Cleared session history for session_id '%s'", session_id)

```

## File: `structural_merger.py`

```python
# utils/structural_merger.py
import logging
from typing import Any, Dict, List, Tuple

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

class StructuralMerger:
    """Isolates building features (doors/windows) from furniture objects list and formats them."""

    def merge_structures(
        self,
        scenes: List[Dict[str, Any]],
        merged_objects: List[Dict[str, Any]]
    ) -> Tuple[List[Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]], List[Dict[str, Any]]]:
        """Scans both the model-identified doors/windows lists and the objects list

        to cleanly classify building structures.

        Returns:
            doors: List of formatted doors
            windows: List of formatted windows
            furniture: List of furniture objects (with structures removed)
        """
        doors_list: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        windows_list: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []
        furniture_objects: List[Dict[str, Any]] = []

        # 1. Collect doors and windows already correctly placed in their respective arrays by individual scenes
        for scene in scenes:
            for d in scene.get("doors", []):
                if d and isinstance(d, dict) and d.get("id") not in [x.get("id") for x in doors_list]:
                    doors_list.append(dict(d))
            for w in scene.get("windows", []):
                if w and isinstance(w, dict) and w.get("id") not in [x.get("id") for x in windows_list]:
                    windows_list.append(dict(w))

        # 2. Inspect merged objects list and isolate misclassified building structures
        for obj in merged_objects:
            name = (obj.get("name") or "").lower()
            category = (obj.get("category") or "").lower()
            subcategory = (obj.get("subcategory") or "").lower()
            combined = f"{name} {category} {subcategory}"

            is_door = any(k in combined for k in ["door", "doorway", "entry", "entrance", "gate", "portal", "exit", "threshold"]) and not any(ex in combined for ex in ["indoor", "outdoor"])
            is_window = any(k in combined for k in ["window", "glass", "pane", "curtain", "drape", "blind", "shutter", "sill", "skylight"])

            if is_door:
                d_id = obj["id"]
                d = {
                    "id": d_id,
                    "type": obj.get("subcategory") or "swing",
                    "opens": "unknown",
                    "estimated_width_cm": obj.get("estimated_dimensions", {}).get("width_cm") or 100.0,
                    "estimated_height_cm": obj.get("estimated_dimensions", {}).get("height_cm") or 200.0,
                    "blocked": False,
                    "bbox": obj.get("bbox", {"x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100})
                }
                if d_id not in [x.get("id") for x in doors_list]:
                    doors_list.append(d)
            elif is_window:
                w_id = obj["id"]
                w = {
                    "id": w_id,
                    "type": obj.get("subcategory") or "sliding",
                    "estimated_width_cm": obj.get("estimated_dimensions", {}).get("width_cm") or 120.0,
                    "estimated_height_cm": obj.get("estimated_dimensions", {}).get("height_cm") or 120.0,
                    "natural_light_contribution": "high" if any(k in str(obj) for k in ["exposure", "light", "sun"]) else "medium",
                    "curtains": True if any(k in str(obj) for k in ["curtain", "drape", "curtains", "drapes"]) else False,
                    "blinds": True if any(k in str(obj) for k in ["blind", "blinds", "shutter", "shutters"]) else False,
                    "glass_type": "clear_glass",
                    "bbox": obj.get("bbox", {"x": 0, "y": 0, "width": 100, "height": 100})
                }
                if w_id not in [x.get("id") for x in windows_list]:
                    windows_list.append(w)
            else:
                furniture_objects.append(obj)

        return doors_list, windows_list, furniture_objects

```
